<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> For youi will not leave my soul in hell; neither will youi suffer youri Holy One to see corruption.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> youi will show me the path of life: in youri presence &#91;is&#93; fullness of joy; at youri right hand &#91;there are&#93; pleasures forevermore.<span className="desc">A Prayer of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Hear the right, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, attend unto my cry, give ear unto my prayer, &#91;that goes&#93; not out of feigned lips.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Let my sentence come forth from youri presence; let youri eyes behold the things that are equal.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> youi have proved my heart; youi have visited &#91;me&#93; in the night; youi have tried me, &#91;and&#93; shall find nothing; I am purposed &#91;that&#93; my mouth shall not transgress.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Concerning the works of men, by the word of youri lips I have kept &#91;myself from&#93; the paths of the destroyer.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Hold up my goings in youri paths, &#91;that&#93; my footsteps slip not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> I have called upon youi, for youi will hear me, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: incline youri ear unto me, &#91;and hear&#93; my speech.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Show youri marvelous loving-kindness, O youi that saves by youri right hand them which put their trust &#91;in youi&#93; from those that rise up &#91;against them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Keep me as the apple of the eye, hide me under the shadow of youri wings,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> From the wicked that oppress me, &#91;from&#93; my deadly enemies, &#91;who&#93; compass (go around, encircle; encompass) me about.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> They are enclosed in their own fat: with their mouth they speak proudly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> They have now compassed us in our steps: they have set their eyes bowing down to the earth;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Like as a lion &#91;that&#93; is greedy of his prey, and as it were a young lion lurking in secret places.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Arise, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, disappoint him, cast him down: deliver my soul from the wicked, &#91;which is&#93; youri sword:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> From men &#91;which are&#93; youri hand, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, from men of the world, &#91;which have&#93; their portion in &#91;this&#93; life, and whose belly youi fill with youri hid &#91;treasure&#93;: they are full of children, and leave the rest of their &#91;substance&#93; to their babes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> As for me, I will behold youri face in righteousness: I shall be satisfied, when I awake, with youri likeness.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, &#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;, the servant of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, who spoke unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; the words of this song in the day &#91;that&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; delivered him from the hand of all his enemies, and from the hand of Sha’ul &#91;Desired&#93;: And he said,</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I will love youi, O Yehovah Khiz’ki &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate My Strength&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Yehovah Sal’i &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate &#123;is&#125; My Rock&#93;, And my fortress, and my deliverer; El Tzuri &#91;The Living Word My Rock&#93;, my strength, in whom I will trust; my buckler (round shielf held with a grip), and the horn of my salvation, &#91;and&#93; my high tower.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> I will call upon the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, &#91;who is worthy&#93; to be praised: so shall I be saved from mine enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> The sorrows of death compassed me, and the floods of ungodly men made me afraid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> The sorrows of hell compassed me about: the snares of death prevented me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> In my distress I called upon the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and cried unto my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: he heard my voice out of his temple, and my cry came before him, &#91;even&#93; into his ears.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Then the earth shook and trembled; the foundations also of the hills moved and were shaken, because he was exceedingly angry.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> There went up a smoke out of his nostrils, and fire out of his mouth devoured: coals were kindled by it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> He bowed the heavens also, and came down: and darkness &#91;was&#93; under his feet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> And he rode upon a cherub, and did fly: yea, he did fly upon the wings of the wind.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> He made darkness his secret place; his pavilion round about him &#91;were&#93; dark waters &#91;and&#93; thick clouds of the skies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> At the brightness &#91;that was&#93; before him his thick clouds passed, hail &#91;stones&#93; and coals of fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; also thundered in the heavens, and the Highest gave his voice; hail &#91;stones&#93; and coals of fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Yea, he sent out his arrows, and scattered them; and he shot out lightnings, and scattered (defeated in battle) them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Then the channels of waters were seen, and the foundations of the world were discovered at youri rebuke (reprimand; strongly warn; restrain), O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, at the blast of the breath of youri nostrils.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> He sent from above, he took me, he drew me out of many waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> He delivered me from my strong enemy, and from them which hated me: for they were too strong for me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> They prevented me in the day of my calamity: but the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; was my stay.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> He brought me forth also into a large place; he delivered me, because he delighted in me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; rewarded me according to my righteousness; according to the cleanness of my hands has he recompensed me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> For I have kept the ways of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and have not wickedly departed from my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> For all his judgments &#91;were&#93; before me, and I did not put away his statutes from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> I was also upright before him, and I kept myself from mine iniquity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Therefore has the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; recompensed me according to my righteousness, according to the cleanness of my hands in his eyesight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> With the merciful youi will show yourself merciful; with an upright man youi will show yourself upright;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> With the pure youi will show yourself pure; and with the perverse (twisted) &#91;habitually disposed to disobedience and opposition&#93; youi will show yourself perverse (twisted) &#91;habitually disposed to disobedience and opposition&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> For youi will save the afflicted people; but will bring down high looks.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> For youi will light my candle: the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; will enlighten my darkness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> For by youi I have run through a troop; and by my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; have I leaped over a wall.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> &#91;As for&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, his way &#91;is&#93; perfect: the word of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; is tried: he &#91;is&#93; a buckler (round shielf held with a grip) to all those that trust in him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> For who &#91;is&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; save the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;? or who &#91;is&#93; a Rock save our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> &#91;It is&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; that girds me with strength, and makes my way perfect.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> He makes my feet like hinds’ &#91;feet&#93;, and sets me upon my high places.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> He teaches my hands to war, so that a bow of steel is broken by mine arms.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> youi have also given me the shield of youri salvation: and youri right hand has held me up, and youri gentleness has made me great.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> youi have enlarged my steps under me, that my feet did not slip.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> I have pursued mine enemies, and overtaken them: neither did I turn again till they were consumed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> I have wounded them that they were not able to rise: they are fallen under my feet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> For youi have girded me with strength unto the battle: youi have subdued under me those that rose up against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> youi have also given me the necks of mine enemies; that I might destroy them that hate me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> They cried, but &#91;there was&#93; none to save &#91;them: even&#93; unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, but he answered them not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> Then did I beat them small as the dust before the wind: I did cast them out as the dirt in the streets.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> youi have delivered me from the strivings of the people; &#91;and&#93; youi have made me the head of the heathen: a people &#91;whom&#93; I have not known shall serve me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> As soon as they hear of me, they shall obey me: the strangers shall submit themselves unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> The strangers shall fade away, and be afraid out of their close places.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; lives; and Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; my Rock; and let the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of my salvation be exalted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> &#91;It is&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; that avenges me, and subdues the people under me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> He delivers me from mine enemies: yea, youi lift me up above those that rise up against me: youi have delivered me from the violent man.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> Therefore will I give thanks unto youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, among the heathen, and sing praises unto youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> Great deliverance gives he to his king; and shows mercy to his anointed, to David &#91;Beloved&#93;, and to his seed forevermore.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> The heavens declare the glory of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; and the Firmament (expanse or vault over the earth) shows his handiwork.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Day unto day utters speech, and night unto night shows knowledge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> &#91;There is&#93; no speech nor language, &#91;where&#93; their voice is not heard.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Their line is gone out through all the earth, and their words to the end of the world. In them has he set a tabernacle for the sun,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Which &#91;is&#93; as a bridegroom coming out of his chamber, &#91;and&#93; rejoices as a strong man to run a race.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> His going forth &#91;is&#93; from the end of the heaven, and his circuit unto the ends of it: and there is nothing hid from the heat thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> The law of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; perfect, converting the soul: the testimony of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; sure, making wise the simple.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> The statutes of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;are&#93; right, rejoicing the heart: the commandment of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; pure, enlightening the eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> The fear of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; clean, enduring forever: the judgments of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;are&#93; true &#91;and&#93; righteous altogether.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> More to be desired &#91;are they&#93; than gold, yea, than much fine gold: sweeter also than honey and the honeycomb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Moreover by them is youri servant warned: &#91;and&#93; in keeping of them &#91;there is&#93; great reward.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Who can understand &#91;his&#93; errors? Cleanse youi me from secret &#91;faults&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Keep back youri servant also from presumptuous &#91;sins&#93;; let them not have dominion over me: then shall I be upright, and I shall be innocent from the great transgression.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Let the words of my mouth, and the meditation of my heart, be acceptable in youri sight, O Yehovah Tzuri &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate My Strength&#93;, and my redeemer.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; hear youi in the day of trouble; the name of the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93; defend youi;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Send youi help from the sanctuary, and strengthen youi out of Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Remember all youri offerings, and accept youri burnt sacrifice; Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Grant youi according to youri own heart, and fulfill all youri counsel.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> We will rejoice in youri salvation, and in the name of our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; we will set up &#91;our&#93; banners: the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; fulfill all youri petitions.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Now know I that the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; saves his anointed; he will hear him from his holy heaven with the saving strength of his right hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Some &#91;trust&#93; in chariots, and some in horses: but we will remember the name of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> They are brought down and fallen: but we are risen, and stand upright.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Save, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: let the king hear us when we call.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> The king shall joy in youri strength, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and in youri salvation how greatly shall he rejoice!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> youi have given him his heart’s desire, and have not withheld the request of his lips. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> For youi prevent (come before; go before; anticipate) him with the blessings of goodness: youi set a crown of pure gold on his head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> He asked life of youi, &#91;and&#93; youi gave &#91;it to&#93; him, &#91;even&#93; length of days forever and ever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> His glory &#91;is&#93; great in youri salvation: honour and majesty have youi laid upon him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> For youi have made him most Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) forever: youi have made him exceeding glad with youri Countenance (Face, appearance; show favor).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> For the king trusts in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and through the mercy of the most High he shall not be moved.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Your1s hand shall find out all youri enemies: youri right hand shall find out those that hate youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> youi shall make them as a fiery oven in the time of youri anger: the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall swallow (to approve, commend, accept) them up in his wrath, and the fire shall devour them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Their fruit shall youi destroy from the earth, and their seed from among the children of men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> For they intended evil against youi: they imagined a mischievous device, &#91;which&#93; they are not able &#91;to perform&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Therefore shall youi make them turn their back, &#91;when&#93; youi shall make ready &#91;youri arrows&#93; upon youri strings against the face of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Be youi exalted, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, in youri own strength: &#91;so&#93; will we sing and praise youri power.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader set to “Doe at Dawn,” A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> My God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, why have youi forsaken me? &#91;why are youi so&#93; far from helping me, &#91;and from&#93; the words of my roaring?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, I cry in the daytime, but youi hear not; and in the night season, and am not silent.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> But youi &#91;are&#93; holy, &#91;O youi&#93; that inhabit the praises of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Our fathers trusted in youi: they trusted, and youi did deliver them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> They cried unto youi, and were delivered: they trusted in youi, and were not confounded.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> But I &#91;am&#93; a worm, and no man; a reproach (disgrace; shame) of men, and despised of the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> All they that see me laugh me to scorn: they shoot out the lip, they shake the head, &#91;saying&#93;,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> He trusted on the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;that&#93; he would deliver him: let him deliver him, seeing he delighted in him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> But youi &#91;are&#93; he that took me out of the womb: youi did make me hope &#91;when I was&#93; upon my mother’s breasts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> I was cast upon youi from the womb: youi &#91;are&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; from my mother’s belly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Be not far from me; for trouble &#91;is&#93; near; for &#91;there is&#93; none to help.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Many bulls have compassed me: strong &#91;bulls&#93; of Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93; have beset me round.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> They gaped upon me &#91;with&#93; their mouths, &#91;as&#93; a ravening (preying upon to devour) and a roaring lion.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> I am poured out like water, and all my bones are out of joint: my heart is like grow (become; advance); it is melted in the midst of my bowels (inward parts; affections).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> My strength is dried up like a potsherd (piece of broken pottery); and my tongue cleaves to my jaws; and youi have brought me into the dust of death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> For dogs have compassed me: the assembly of the wicked have enclosed me: they pierced my hands and my feet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> I may tell all my bones: they look &#91;and&#93; stare upon me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> They part my garments among them, and cast lots upon my vesture.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> But be not youi far from me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: O my strength, haste youi to help me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Deliver my soul from the sword; my darling from the power of the dog.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Save me from the lion’s mouth: for youi have heard me from the horns of the unicorns.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> I will declare youri name unto my brethren: in the midst of the congregation will I praise youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> You2f that fear the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, praise him; all you2f the seed of Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;, glorify him; and fear him, all you2f the seed of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> For he has not despised nor abhorred the affliction of the afflicted; neither has he hid his face from him; but when he cried unto him, he heard.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> My praise &#91;shall be&#93; of youi in the great congregation: I will pay my vows before them that fear him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> The meek shall eat and be satisfied: they shall praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; that seek him: your heart shall live forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> All the ends of the world shall remember and turn unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: and all the kindreds of the nations shall worship before youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> For the kingdom &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: and he &#91;is&#93; the governor among the nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> All &#91;they that be&#93; fat upon earth shall eat and worship: all they that go down to the dust shall bow before him: and none can keep alive his own soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> A seed shall serve him; it shall be accounted to the Lord for a generation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> They shall come, and shall declare his righteousness unto a people that shall be born, that he has done &#91;this&#93;.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Yehovah Ro’i &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate My shepherd&#93;; I shall not lack.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> He makes me to lie down in green pastures: he leads me beside the still waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> He restores my soul: he leads me in the paths of righteousness for his name’s sake.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Yea, though I walk through the valley of the shadow of death, I will fear no evil: for youi &#91;are&#93; with me; youri rod and youri staff they comfort me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> youi prepare a table before me in the presence of mine enemies: youi anoint my head with oil; my cup runs over.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Surely goodness and mercy shall follow me all the days of my life: and I will dwell in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; forever.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> The earth &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and the fullness thereof; the world, and they that dwell therein.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> For he has founded it upon the seas, and established it upon the floods.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Who shall ascend into the hill of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;? or who shall stand in his holy place?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> He that has clean hands, and a pure heart; who has not lifted up his soul unto vanity, nor sworn deceitfully.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> He shall receive the blessing from the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and righteousness from the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of his salvation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> This &#91;is&#93; the generation of them that seek him, that seek youri face, O Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Lift up your heads, O you2f gates; and be you2f lift up, you2f everlasting doors; and Melekh HaKavod &#91;the King of Glory&#93; shall come in!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Who &#91;is&#93; this King of glory? Yehovah ‘Ezuz v’Gibor &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate Strong and Mighty&#93;, Yehovah Gibor Mil’khamah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate Mighty in Battle&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Lift up your heads, O you2f gates; even lift &#91;them&#93; up, you2f everlasting doors; and Melekh HaKavod &#91;the King of Glory&#93; shall come in!.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Who is this King of glory? The Yehovah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, he &#91;is&#93; Melekh HaKavod &#91;the King of Glory&#93;. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93; &#91;Diligently stop and consider what was just said&#93;.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Unto youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, do I lift up my soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, I trust in youi: let me not be ashamed, let not mine enemies triumph over me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Yea, let none that wait on youi be ashamed: let them be ashamed which transgress without cause.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Show me youri ways, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; teach me youi paths.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Lead me in youri truth, and teach me: for youi &#91;are&#93; the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of my salvation; on youi do I wait all the day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Remember, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, youri tender mercies and youi lovingkindnesses; for they &#91;have been&#93; ever of old.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Remember not the sins of my youth, nor my transgressions: according to youri mercy remember youi me for youri goodness’ sake, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Good and upright &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: therefore will he teach sinners in the way.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> The meek will he guide in judgment: and the meek will he teach his way.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> All the paths of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;are&#93; mercy and tunto such as keep his covenant and his testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> For youri name’s sake, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, pardon mine iniquity; for it &#91;is&#93; great.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> What man &#91;is&#93; he that fears the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;? him shall he teach in the way &#91;that&#93; he shall choose.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> His soul shall dwell at ease; and his seed shall inherit the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> The secret of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; with them that fear him; and he will show them his covenant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Mine eyes &#91;are&#93; ever toward the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; for he shall pluck my feet out of the net.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Turn youi unto me, and have mercy upon me; for I &#91;am&#93; desolate and afflicted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> The troubles of my heart are enlarged: &#91;O&#93; bring youi me out of my distresses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Look upon mine affliction and my pain; and forgive all my sins.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Consider mine enemies; for they are many; and they hate me with cruel hatred.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> O keep my soul, and deliver me: let me not be ashamed; for I put my trust in youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Let integrity and uprightness preserve me; for I wait on youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Redeem Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, out of all his troubles.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Judge me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; for I have walked in mine integrity: I have trusted also in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; &#91;therefore&#93; I shall not slide.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Examine me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and prove me; try my reins (literally, kidneys; figuratively, seat of emotion) and my heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> For youri lovingkindness &#91;is&#93; before mine eyes: and I have walked in youri truth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> I have not sat with vain persons, neither will I go in with dissemblers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> I have hated the congregation of evil doers; and will not sit with the wicked.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> I will wash mine hands in innocence: so will I compass (go around, encircle; encompass) youri altar, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> That I may publish with the voice of thanksgiving, and tell of all youri wondrous works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, I have loved the habitation of youri house, and the place where youri honour dwells.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Gather not my soul with sinners, nor my life with bloody men:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> In whose hands &#91;is&#93; mischief, and their right hand is full of bribes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> But as for me, I will walk in mine integrity: redeem me, and be merciful unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> My foot stands in an even place: in the congregations will I bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; my light and my salvation; whom shall I fear? the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; the strength of my life; of whom shall I be afraid?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> When the wicked, &#91;even&#93; mine enemies and my foes, came upon me to eat up my flesh, they stumbled and fell.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Though an host should encamp against me, my heart shall not fear: though war should rise against me, in this &#91;will&#93; I &#91;be&#93; confident.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> One &#91;thing&#93; have I desired of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, that will I seek after; that I may dwell in the house of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; all the days of my life, to behold the beauty of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and to enquire in his temple.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> &#91;When youi said&#93;, Seek you2f my face; my heart said unto youi, Your1s face, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, will I seek.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Hide not youri face &#91;far&#93; from me; put not youri servant away in anger: youi have been my help; leave me not, neither forsake me, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of my salvation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Teach me youri way, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and lead me in a plain path, because of mine enemies.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Deliver me not over unto the will of mine enemies: for false witnesses are risen up against me, and such as breathe out cruelty.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Wait on the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: be of good courage, and he shall strengthen youri heart: wait, I say, on the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Unto youi will I cry, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; my rock; be not silent to me: lest, &#91;if&#93; youi be silent to me, I become like them that go down into the pit.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Draw me not away with the wicked, and with the workers of iniquity, which speak peace to their neighbours, but mischief &#91;is&#93; in their hearts.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Into youri hand I commit my spirit: youi have redeemed me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; God-El Emet &#91;The Living Word of Truth&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> I have hated them that regard lying vanities: but I trust in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> I will be glad and rejoice in youri mercy: for youi have considered my trouble; youi have known my soul in adversities;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> And have not shut me up into the hand of the enemy: youi have set my feet in a large room.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Have mercy upon me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, for I am in trouble: mine eye is consumed with grief, &#91;yea&#93;, my soul and my belly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> For my life is spent with grief, and my years with sighing: my strength fails because of mine iniquity, and my bones are consumed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> I was a reproach (disgrace; shame) among all mine enemies, but especially among my neighbours, and a fear to mine acquaintance: they that did see me without fled from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> I am forgotten as a dead man out of mind: I am like a broken vessel.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> For I have heard the slander of many: fear &#91;was&#93; on every side: while they took counsel together against me, they devised to take away my life.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> But I trusted in youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: I said, youi &#91;are&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> My times &#91;are&#93; in youri hand: deliver me from the hand of mine enemies, and from them that persecute me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Make youri face to shine upon youri servant: save me for youri mercies’ sake.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> Let me not be ashamed, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; for I have called upon youi: let the wicked be ashamed, &#91;and&#93; let them be silent in the grave.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Let the lying lips be put to silence; which speak grievous things proudly and contemptuously against the righteous.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> &#91;Oh&#93; how great &#91;is&#93; youri goodness, which youi have laid up for them that fear youi; &#91;which&#93; youi have wrought for them that trust in youi before the sons of men!</span>
</div>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: for he has showed me his marvelous kindness in a strong city.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> For I said in my haste, I am cut off from before youri eyes: nevertheless youi heard the voice of my supplications when I cried unto youi.</span>

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> He loves righteousness and judgment: the earth is full of the goodness of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> By the word of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; were the heavens made; and all the host of them by the breath of his mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
