</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Let all the earth fear the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: let all the inhabitants of the world stand in awe of him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> For he spoke, and it was &#91;done&#93;; he commanded, and it stood fast.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; brings the counsel of the heathen to nothing (bad; vain): he makes the devices of the people of none effect.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> The counsel of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; stands forever, the thoughts of his heart to all generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;is&#93; the nation whose God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; &#91;and&#93; the people &#91;whom&#93; he has chosen for his own inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; looks from heaven; he beholds all the sons of men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> From the place of his habitation he looks upon all the inhabitants of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> He fashions their hearts alike; he considers all their works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> There is no king saved by the multitude of an host: a mighty man is not delivered by much strength.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> An horse &#91;is&#93; a vain thing for safety: neither shall he deliver &#91;any&#93; by his great strength.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Behold, the eye of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; upon them that fear him, upon them that hope in his mercy;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> To deliver their soul from death, and to keep them alive in famine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Our soul waits for the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: he &#91;is&#93; our help and our shield.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> For our heart shall rejoice in him, because we have trusted in his holy name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Let youri mercy, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, be upon us, according as we hope in youi.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;, when he changed his behaviour before Avi’Melekh &#91;My Father is King&#93;; who drove him away, and he departed.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I will bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; at all times: his praise &#91;shall&#93; continually &#91;be&#93; in my mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> My soul shall make her boast in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: the humble shall hear &#91;thereof&#93;, and be glad.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> O magnify the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; with me, and let us exalt his name together.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> I sought the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and he heard me, and delivered me from all my fears.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> They looked unto him, and were lightened: and their faces were not ashamed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> This poor man cried, and the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; heard &#91;him&#93;, and saved him out of all his troubles.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> The angel of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; encamps round about them that fear him, and delivers them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> O taste and see that the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; good: Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;is&#93; the man &#91;that&#93; trusts in him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> O fear the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, you2f his saints: for &#91;there is&#93; no lack to them that fear him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> The young lions do lack, and suffer hunger: but they that seek the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall not lack any good &#91;thing&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Come, you2f children, hearken unto me: I will teach you the fear of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> What man &#91;is he that&#93; desires life, &#91;and&#93; loves &#91;many&#93; days, that he may see good?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Keep youri tongue from evil, and youri lips from speaking deceit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Depart from evil, and do good; seek peace, and pursue it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> The eyes of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;are&#93; upon the righteous, and his ears &#91;are open&#93; unto their cry.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> The face of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; against them that do evil, to cut off the remembrance of them from the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> &#91;The righteous&#93; cry, and the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; hears, and delivers them out of all their troubles.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; near unto them that are of a broken heart; and saves such as be of a contrite spirit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Many &#91;are&#93; the afflictions of the righteous: but the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; delivers him out of them all.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> He keeps all his bones: not one of them is broken.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Evil shall slay the wicked: and they that hate the righteous shall be desolate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; redeems the soul of his servants: and none of them that trust in him shall be desolate.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Plead &#91;my cause&#93;, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, with them that strive with me: fight against them that fight against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Take hold of shield and buckler (round shielf held with a grip), and stand up for mine help.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Draw out also the spear, and stop &#91;the way&#93; against them that persecute me: say unto my soul, I &#91;am&#93; youri salvation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Let them be confounded and put to shame that seek after my soul: let them be turned back and brought to confusion that devise my hurt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Let them be as chaff before the wind: and let the angel of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; chase &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Let their way be dark and slippery: and let the angel of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; persecute them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> For without cause have they hid for me their net &#91;in&#93; a pit, &#91;which&#93; without cause they have digged for my soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Let destruction come upon him at unawares; and let his net that he has hid catch himself: into that very destruction let him fall.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> And my soul shall be joyful in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: it shall rejoice in his salvation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> All my bones shall say, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, who &#91;is&#93; like unto youi, which delivers the poor from him that is too strong for him, yea, the poor and the needy from him that spoils him?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> False witnesses did rise up; they laid to my charge &#91;things&#93; that I knew not.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> They rewarded me evil for good &#91;to&#93; the spoiling of my soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> But as for me, when they were sick, my clothing &#91;was&#93; sackcloth: I humbled my soul with fasting; and my prayer returned into mine own bosom.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> I behaved myself as though &#91;he had been&#93; my friend &#91;or&#93; brother: I bowed down heavily, as one that mourns &#91;for his&#93; mother.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> But in mine adversity they rejoiced, and gathered themselves together: &#91;yea&#93;, the outcasts gathered themselves together against me, and I knew &#91;it&#93; not; they did tear &#91;me&#93;, and ceased not:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> With hypocritical mockers in feasts, they gnashed upon me with their teeth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, how long will youi look on? rescue my soul from their destructions, my darling from the lions.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> I will give youi thanks in the great congregation: I will praise youi among much people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Let not them that are mine enemies wrongfully rejoice over me: &#91;neither&#93; let them wink with the eye that hate me without a cause.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> For they speak not peace: but they devise deceitful matters against &#91;them that are&#93; quiet in the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Yea, they opened their mouth wide against me, &#91;and&#93; said, Aha, aha, our eye has seen &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> &#91;This&#93; youi have seen, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: keep not silence: O Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, be not far from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Stir up yourself, and awake to my judgment, &#91;even&#93; unto my cause, my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; and my Lord.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Judge me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, according to youri righteousness; and let them not rejoice over me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Let them not say in their hearts, Ah, so would we have it: let them not say, We have swallow (to approve, commend, accept)ed him up.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Let them be ashamed and brought to confusion together that rejoice at mine hurt: let them be clothed with shame and dishonour that magnify &#91;themselves&#93; against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Let them shout for joy, and be glad, that favour my righteous cause: yea, let them say continually, Let the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; be magnified, which has pleasure in the prosperity of his servant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And my tongue shall speak of youri righteousness &#91;and&#93; of youri praise all the day long.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, &#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93; the servant of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> The transgression of the wicked says within my heart, &#91;that there is&#93; no fear of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; before his eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> For he flatters himself in his own eyes, until his iniquity be found to be hateful.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> The words of his mouth &#91;are&#93; iniquity and deceit: he has left off to be wise, &#91;and&#93; to do good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> He devises mischief upon his bed; he sets himself in a way &#91;that is&#93; not good; he abhors not evil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Your2s mercy, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, &#91;is&#93; in the heavens; &#91;and&#93; youi faithfulness &#91;reaches&#93; unto the clouds.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Your2s righteousness &#91;is&#93; like the great mountains; youri judgments &#91;are&#93; a great deep: O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, youi preserve man and beast.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> How excellent &#91;is&#93; youri lovingkindness, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;! therefore the children of men put their trust under the shadow of youri wings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> They shall be abundantly satisfied with the fatness of youri house; and youi shall make them drink of the river of youri pleasures.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> For with youi &#91;is&#93; the fountain of life: in youri light shall we see light.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> O continue youri lovingkindness unto them that know youi; and youri righteousness to the upright in heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Let not the foot of pride come against me, and let not the hand of the wicked remove me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> There are the workers of iniquity fallen: they are cast down, and shall not be able to rise.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Be troubled (be grieved; be displeased) not yourself because of evildoers, neither be youi envious against the workers of iniquity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> For they shall soon be cut down like the grass, and wither as the green herb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Trust in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and do good; &#91;so&#93; shall youi dwell in the land, and truly youi shall be fed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Delight yourself also in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and he shall give youi the desires of youi heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Commit youri way unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; trust also in him; and he shall bring &#91;it&#93; to pass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> And he shall bring forth youri righteousness as the light, and youri judgment as the noonday.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Rest in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and wait patiently for him: be troubled (be grieved; be displeased) not yourself because of him who prospers in his way, because of the man who brings wicked devices to pass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Cease from anger, and forsake wrath: be troubled (be grieved; be displeased) not yourself in any wise to do evil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> For evildoers shall be cut off: but those that wait upon the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, they shall inherit the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> For yet a little while, and the wicked &#91;shall&#93; not &#91;be&#93;: yea, youi shall diligently consider his place, and it &#91;shall&#93; not &#91;be&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> But the meek shall inherit the earth; and shall delight themselves in the abundance of peace.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> The wicked plots against the just, and gnashes upon him with his teeth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> The Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; shall laugh at him: for he sees that his day is coming.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> The wicked have drawn out the sword, and have bent their bow, to cast down the poor and needy, &#91;and&#93; to slay such as be of upright Conversation (behavior; way of life; community).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Their sword shall enter into their own heart, and their bows shall be broken.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> A little that a righteous man has &#91;is&#93; better than the riches of many wicked.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> For the arms of the wicked shall be broken: but the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; upholds the righteous.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; knows the days of the upright: and their inheritance shall be forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> They shall not be ashamed in the evil time: and in the days of famine they shall be satisfied.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> But the wicked shall perish, and the enemies of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;shall be&#93; as the fat of lambs: they shall consume; into smoke shall they consume away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> The wicked borrows, and pays not again: but the righteous shows mercy, and gives.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> For &#91;such as be&#93; Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) of him shall inherit the earth; and &#91;they that be&#93; cursed of him shall be cut off.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> The steps of a &#91;good&#93; man are ordered by the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: and he delights in his way.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Though he fall, he shall not be utterly cast down: for the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; upholds &#91;him with&#93; his hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> I have been young, and &#91;now&#93; am old; yet have I not seen the righteous forsaken, nor his seed begging bread.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> &#91;He is&#93; ever merciful, and lends; and his seed &#91;is&#93; Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Depart from evil, and do good; and dwell forevermore.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> For the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; loves judgment, and forsakes not his saints; they are preserved forever: but the seed of the wicked shall be cut off.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> The righteous shall inherit the land, and dwell therein forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> The mouth of the righteous speaks wisdom, and his tongue talks of judgment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> The law of his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; in his heart; none of his steps shall slide.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> The wicked watches the righteous, and seeks to slay him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will not leave him in his hand, nor condemn him when he is judged.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Wait on the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and keep his way, and he shall exalt youi to inherit the land: when the wicked are cut off, youi shall see &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> I have seen the wicked in great power, and spreading himself like a green bay tree.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> Yet he passed away, and, lo, he &#91;was&#93; not: yea, I sought him, but he could not be found.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Mark the perfect &#91;man&#93;, and behold the upright: for the end of &#91;that&#93; man &#91;is&#93; peace.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> But the transgressors shall be destroyed together: the end of the wicked shall be cut off.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> But the salvation of the righteous &#91;is&#93; of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: &#91;he is&#93; their strength in the time of trouble.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> And the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall help them, and deliver them: he shall deliver them from the wicked, and save them, because they trust in him.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;, to bring to remembrance.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, rebuke (reprimand; strongly warn; restrain) me not in youri wrath: neither chasten me in youri hot displeasure.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> For youri arrows stick fast in me, and youri hand presses me sore.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> &#91;There is&#93; no soundness in my flesh because of youri anger; neither &#91;is there any&#93; rest in my bones because of my sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> For mine iniquities are gone over mine head: as an heavy burden they are too heavy for me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> My wounds stink &#91;and&#93; are corrupt because of my foolishness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> I am troubled; I am bowed down greatly; I go mourning all the day long.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> For my loins (thigh and groin area; often used for the reproductive organs) are filled with a loathsome &#91;disease&#93;: and &#91;there is&#93; no soundness in my flesh.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> I am feeble and sore broken: I have roared by reason of the disquietness of my heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, all my desire &#91;is&#93; before youi; and my groaning is not hid from youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> My heart pants, my strength fails me: as for the light of mine eyes, it also is gone from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> My lovers and my friends stand aloof (at a distance, but within view) from my sore; and my kinsmen stand afar off.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> They also that seek after my life lay snares &#91;for me&#93;: and they that seek my hurt speak mischievous things, and imagine deceits all the day long.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> But I, as a deaf &#91;man&#93;, heard not; and &#91;I was&#93; as a dumb man &#91;that&#93; opens not his mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Thus I was as a man that hears not, and in whose mouth &#91;are&#93; no reproofs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> For in youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, do I hope: youi will hear, O Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; my God-Elohai &#91;GOD&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> For I said, &#91;Hear me&#93;, lest &#91;otherwise&#93; they should rejoice over me: when my foot slips, they magnify &#91;themselves&#93; against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> For I &#91;am&#93; ready to lame (crippled in the feet), and my sorrow &#91;is&#93; continually before me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> For I will declare mine iniquity; I will be sorry for my sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> But mine enemies &#91;are&#93; lively, &#91;and&#93; they are strong: and they that hate me wrongfully are multiplied.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> They also that render evil for good are mine adversaries; because I follow &#91;the thing that&#93; good &#91;is&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Forsake me not, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, be not far from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Make haste to help me, O Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; my salvation.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, &#91;even&#93; to Y’dutun &#91;Praising&#93;, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I said, I will take heed to my ways, that I sin not with my tongue: I will keep my mouth with a bridle, while the wicked is before me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> I was dumb with silence, I held my peace, &#91;even&#93; from good; and my sorrow was stirred.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> My heart was hot within me, while I was musing the fire burned: &#91;then&#93; spoke I with my tongue,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, make me to know mine end, and the measure of my days, what it &#91;is; that&#93; I may know how frail I &#91;am&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Behold, youi have made my days &#91;as&#93; an handbreadth; and mine age &#91;is&#93; as nothing before youi: truly every man at his best state &#91;is&#93; altogether vanity. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Surely every man walks in a vain show: surely they are disquieted in vain: he heaps up &#91;riches&#93;, and knows not who shall gather them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> And now, Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, what wait I for? my hope &#91;is&#93; in youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Deliver me from all my transgressions: make me not the reproach (disgrace; shame) of the foolish.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> I was dumb, I opened not my mouth; because youi did &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Remove youri stroke away from me: I am consumed by the blow of youri hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> When youi with rebukes do correct man for iniquity, youi make his beauty to consume away like a moth: surely every man &#91;is&#93; vanity. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Hear my prayer, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and give ear unto my cry; hold not youri peace at my tears: for I &#91;am&#93; a stranger with youi, &#91;and&#93; a sojourner, as all my fathers &#91;were&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> O spare me, that I may recover strength, before I go hence, and be no more.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I waited patiently for the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and he inclined unto me, and heard my cry.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> He brought me up also out of an horrible pit, out of the miry clay, and set my feet upon a rock, &#91;and&#93; established my goings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> And he has put a new song in my mouth, &#91;even&#93; praise unto our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: many shall see &#91;it&#93;, and fear, and shall trust in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;is&#93; that man that makes the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; his trust, and respects not the proud, nor such as turn aside to lies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Many, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;are&#93; youri wonderful works &#91;which&#93; youi have done, and youri thoughts &#91;which are&#93; toward us: they cannot be reckoned up in order unto youi: &#91;if&#93; I would declare and speak &#91;of them&#93;, they are more than can be numbered.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Sacrifice and offering youi did not desire; mine ears have youi opened: burnt offering and sin offering have youi not required.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Then said I, Lo, I come: in the volume of the book &#91;it is&#93; written of me,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> I delight to do youi will, O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: yea, youri law &#91;is&#93; within my heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> I have preached righteousness in the great congregation: lo, I have not refrained my lips, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, youi know.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> I have not hid youri righteousness within my heart; I have declared youi faithfulness and youi salvation: I have not concealed youri lovingkindness and youri tfrom the great congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Withhold not youi youri tender mercies from me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: let youri lovingkindness and youri tcontinually preserve me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> For innumerable evils have compassed me about: mine iniquities have taken hold upon me, so that I am not able to look up; they are more than the hairs of mine head: therefore my heart fails me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Be pleased, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, to deliver me: O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, make haste to help me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Let them be ashamed and confounded together that seek after my soul to destroy it; let them be driven backward and put to shame that wish me evil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Let them be desolate for a reward of their shame that say unto me, Aha, aha.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Let all those that seek youi rejoice and be glad in youi: let such as love youi salvation say continually, The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; be magnified.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> But I &#91;am&#93; poor and needy; &#91;yet&#93; the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; thinks upon me: youi &#91;are&#93; my help and my deliverer; make no tarrying, O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;is&#93; he that considers the poor: the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will deliver him in time of trouble.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will preserve him, and keep him alive; &#91;and&#93; he shall be Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) upon the earth: and youi will not deliver him unto the will of his enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will strengthen him upon the bed of languishing: youi will make all his bed in his sickness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> I said, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, be merciful unto me: heal my soul; for I have sinned against youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Mine enemies speak evil of me, When shall he die, and his name perish?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> And if he come to see &#91;me&#93;, he speaks vanity: his heart gathers iniquity to itself; &#91;when&#93; he goes abroad, he tells &#91;it&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> All that hate me whisper together against me: against me do they devise my hurt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> An evil disease, &#91;say they&#93;, cleaves fast unto him: and &#91;now&#93; that he lies he shall rise up no more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Yea, mine own familiar friend, in whom I trusted, which did eat of my bread, has lifted up &#91;his&#93; heel against me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> But youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, be merciful unto me, and raise me up, that I may requite (repay) them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> By this I know that youi favour me, because mine enemy does not triumph over me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> And as for me, youi uphold me in mine integrity, and set me before youri face forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; God-Elohe (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; from everlasting, and to everlasting. Amen, and Amen.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, Maskhil &#91;a contemplative poem&#93;, for the sons of Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> As the deer pants after the water brooks, so pants my soul after youi, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> My soul thirsts for God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, for El Chai &#91;The Powerful Living Word of God&#93;: when shall I come and appear before God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> My tears have been my meat day and night, while they continually say unto me, Where &#91;is&#93; youri God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> When I remember these &#91;things&#93;, I pour out my soul in me: for I had gone with the multitude, I went with them to the house of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, with the voice of joy and praise, with a multitude that kept holyday.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Why are youi cast down, O my soul? and &#91;why&#93; are youi disquieted in me? hope youi in God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: for I shall yet praise him &#91;for&#93; the help of his Countenance (Face, appearance; show favor).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, my soul is cast down within me: therefore will I remember youi from the land of Yarden &#91;Descender&#93;, and of the Hermonim &#91;a sanctuary&#93;, from the hill Mizar &#91;small&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Deep calls unto deep at the noise of youri waterspouts: all youri waves and youri billows are gone over me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> &#91;Yet&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will command his lovingkindness in the daytime, and in the night his song &#91;shall be&#93; with me, &#91;and&#93; my prayer unto the God-El Chai’i &#91;The Powerful Living Word of My Life&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> I will say unto God-El Sal’i &#91;The Powerful Living Word My Rock&#93;, Why have youi forgotten me? why go I mourning because of the oppression of the enemy?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> &#91;As&#93; with a sword in my bones, mine enemies reproach (disgrace; shame) me; while they say daily unto me, Where &#91;is&#93; youri God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> We have heard with our ears, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, our fathers have told us, &#91;what&#93; work youi did in their days, in the times of old.</span>

<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> youi are my King, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: command deliverances for Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Through youi will we push down our enemies: through youri name will we tread them under that rise up against us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> For I will not trust in my bow, neither shall my sword save me.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> But youi have saved us from our enemies, and have put them to shame that hated us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> In God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; we boast all the day long, and praise youri name forever. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> But youi have cast off, and put us to shame; and go not forth with our armies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> youi make us a reproach (disgrace; shame) to our neighbours, a scorn and a ridicule (laughing at enemies’ threats) to them that are round about us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> youi make us a byword among the heathen, a shaking of the head among the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> My confusion &#91;is&#93; continually before me, and the shame of my face has covered me,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> For the voice of him that reproaches and blasphemes; by reason of the enemy and avenger.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> All this is come upon us; yet have we not forgotten youi, neither have we dealt falsely in youri covenant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Trust in him at all times; &#91;you2f&#93; people, pour out your heart before him: God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; a refuge for us. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Surely men of low degree &#91;are&#93; vanity, &#91;and&#93; men of high degree &#91;are&#93; a lie: to be laid in the balance, they &#91;are&#93; altogether &#91;lighter&#93; than vanity.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Also unto youi, O Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, &#91;belongs&#93; mercy: for youi render to every man according to his work.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;, when he was in the wilderness of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;.</span></span>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, youi &#91;are&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; early will I seek youi: my soul thirsts for youi, my flesh longs for youi in a dry and thirsty land, where no water is;</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> The earth shook, the heavens also dropped at the presence of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: &#91;even&#93; Sinai itself &#91;was moved&#93; at the presence of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> youi, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, did send a plentiful rain, whereby youi did confirm youri inheritance, when it was weary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Your1s congregation has dwelt therein: youi, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, have prepared of youri goodness for the poor.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Why leap you2f, you2f high hills? &#91;this is&#93; the hill &#91;which&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; desires to dwell in; yea, the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will dwell &#91;in it&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> The chariots of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;are&#93; twenty thousand, &#91;even&#93; thousands of angels: the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; &#91;is&#93; among them, &#91;as in&#93; Sinai, in the holy &#91;place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> youi have ascended on high, youi have led captivity captive: youi have received gifts for men; yea, &#91;for&#93; the rebellious also, that the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; might dwell &#91;among them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, &#91;who&#93; daily loads us &#91;with benefits, even&#93; the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of our salvation. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> &#91;He that is&#93; our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of salvation; and unto GOD the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; &#91;belong&#93; the issues from death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> But God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; shall wound the head of his enemies, &#91;and&#93; the hairy scalp of such an one as goes on still in his trespasses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> The Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; said, I will bring again from Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93;, I will bring &#91;my people&#93; again from the depths of the sea:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> They have seen youri goings, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; &#91;even&#93; the goings of my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, my King, in the sanctuary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> The singers went before, the players on instruments &#91;followed&#93; after; among &#91;them were&#93; the damsels playing with timbrels.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Bless you2f God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in the congregations, &#91;even&#93; the LORD &#91;Yehovah&#93;, from the fountain of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> There &#91;is&#93; little Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93; &#91;with&#93; their ruler, the princes of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; &#91;and&#93; their council, the princes of Z’vulun &#91;Glorious Dwelling Place&#93;, &#91;and&#93; the princes of Naftali &#91;My Wrestling&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Your2s God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has commanded youri strength: strengthen, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, that which youi have wrought for us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Because of youri temple at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93; shall kings bring presents unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Rebuke (reprimand; strongly warn; restrain) the company of spearmen, the multitude of the bulls, with the calves of the people, &#91;till every one&#93; submit himself with pieces of silver: scatter youi the people &#91;that&#93; delight in war.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;youi are&#93; terrible out of youri holy places: the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; &#91;is&#93; he that gives strength and power unto &#91;his&#93; people. Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader upon Shoshannim, &#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Save me, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; for the waters are come in unto &#91;my&#93; soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> I sink in deep mire, where &#91;there is&#93; no standing: I am come into deep waters, where the floods overflow me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> I am weary of my crying: my throat is dried: mine eyes fail while I wait for my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> They that hate me without a cause are more than the hairs of mine head: they that would destroy me, &#91;being&#93; mine enemies wrongfully, are mighty: then I restored &#91;that&#93; which I took not away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, youi know my foolishness; and my sins are not hid from youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Let not them that wait on youi, O Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih &#91;THE FATHER&#93; Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, be ashamed for my sake: let not those that seek youi be confounded for my sake, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Because for youri sake I have borne reproach (disgrace; shame); shame has covered my face.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> I am become a stranger unto my brethren, and an alien unto my mother’s children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> For the zeal of youri house has eaten me up; and the reproaches of them that reproached youi are fallen upon me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> When I wept, &#91;and chastened&#93; my soul with fasting, that was to my reproach (disgrace; shame).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> I made sackcloth also my garment; and I became a proverb to them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> They that sit in the gate speak against me; and I &#91;was&#93; the song of the drunkards.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> But as for me, my prayer &#91;is&#93; unto youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, &#91;in&#93; an acceptable time: O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, in the multitude of youri mercy hear me, in the tof youri salvation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Deliver me out of the mire, and let me not sink: let me be delivered from them that hate me, and out of the deep waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Let not the waterflood overflow me, neither let the deep swallow (to approve, commend, accept) me up, and let not the pit shut her mouth upon me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Hear me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; for youri lovingkindness &#91;is&#93; good: turn unto me according to the multitude of youri tender mercies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> And hide not youri face from youri servant; for I am in trouble: hear me speedily.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Draw near unto my soul, &#91;and&#93; redeem it: deliver me because of mine enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> youi have known my reproach (disgrace; shame), and my shame, and my dishonour: mine adversaries &#91;are&#93; all before youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Reproach (disgrace; shame) has broken my heart; and I am full of heaviness: and I looked &#91;for some&#93; to take pity, but &#91;there was&#93; none; and for comforters, but I found none.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> They gave me also gall (something bitter or poisonous) for my meat; and in my thirst they gave me vinegar to drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Let their table become a snare before them: and &#91;that which should have been&#93; for &#91;their&#93; welfare, &#91;let it become&#93; a trap.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Let their eyes be darkened, that they see not; and make their loins (thigh and groin area; often used for the reproductive organs) continually to shake.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Pour out youri indignation upon them, and let youri wrathful anger take hold of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> For they persecute &#91;him&#93; whom youi have smitten; and they talk to the grief of those whom youi have wounded.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Add iniquity unto their iniquity: and let them not come into youri righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Let them be blotted out of the book of the living, and not be written with the righteous.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> But I &#91;am&#93; poor and sorrowful: let youri salvation, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, set me up on high.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> I will praise the name of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; with a song, and will magnify him with thanksgiving.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> &#91;This&#93; also shall please the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; better than an ox &#91;or&#93; bullock that has horns and hoofs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> The humble shall see &#91;this, and&#93; be glad: and your heart shall live that seek God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> For the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; hears the poor, and despises not his prisoners.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Let the heaven and earth praise him, the seas, and everything that moves therein.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> For God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; will save Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;, and will build the cities of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;: that they may dwell there, and have it in possession.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> The seed also of his servants shall inherit it: and they that love his name shall dwell therein.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, &#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;, to bring to remembrance.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> &#91;Make haste&#93;, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, to deliver me; make haste to help me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Let them be ashamed and confounded that seek after my soul: let them be turned backward, and put to confusion, that desire my hurt.</span>
</div>
