<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Let them be turned back for a reward of their shame that say, Aha, aha.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Let all those that seek youi rejoice and be glad in youi: and let such as love youri salvation say continually, Let God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; be magnified.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> But I &#91;am&#93; poor and needy: make haste unto me, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: youi &#91;are&#93; my help and my deliverer; O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, make no tarrying.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> In youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, do I put my trust: let me never be put to confusion.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Deliver me in youri righteousness, and cause me to escape: incline youri ear unto me, and save me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Be youi my strong habitation, whereunto I may continually resort: youi have given commandment to save me; for youi &#91;are&#93; my rock and my fortress.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Deliver me, O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, out of the hand of the wicked, out of the hand of the unrighteous and cruel man.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> For youi &#91;are&#93; my hope, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih &#91;THE FATHER&#93;: &#91;youi are&#93; my trust from my youth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> By youi have I been held up from the womb: youi are he that took me out of my mother’s bowels (inward parts; affections): my praise &#91;shall be&#93; continually of youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> I am as a wonder unto many; but youi &#91;are&#93; my strong refuge.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Let my mouth be filled &#91;with&#93; youri praise &#91;and with&#93; youri honour all the day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Cast me not off in the time of old age; forsake me not when my strength fails.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> For mine enemies speak against me; and they that lay wait for my soul take counsel together,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Saying, God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has forsaken him: persecute and take him; for &#91;there is&#93; none to deliver &#91;him&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, be not far from me: O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, make haste for my help.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Let them be confounded &#91;and&#93; consumed that are adversaries to my soul; let them be covered &#91;with&#93; reproach (disgrace; shame) and dishonour that seek my hurt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> But I will hope continually, and will yet praise youi more and more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> My mouth shall show forth youri righteousness &#91;and&#93; youri salvation all the day; for I know not the &#91;thereof&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> I will go in the strength of the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih &#91;THE FATHER&#93;: I will make mention of youri righteousness, &#91;even&#93; of youi only.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, youi have taught me from my youth: and till this point in time have I declared youri wondrous works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Now also when I am old and grayheaded, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, forsake me not; until I have showed youri strength unto &#91;this&#93; generation, &#91;and&#93; youri power to every one &#91;that&#93; is to come.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Your2s righteousness also, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;is&#93; very high, who have done great things: O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, who &#91;is&#93; like unto youi!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> &#91;youi&#93;, which have showed me great and sore troubles, shall quicken me again, and shall bring me up again from the depths of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> youi shall increase my greatness, and comfort me on every side.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> I will also praise youi with the Psaltery (stringed instrument, like an harp), &#91;even&#93; youri truth, O my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: unto youi will I sing with the harp, O youi Holy One of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> My lips shall greatly rejoice when I sing unto youi; and my soul, which youi have redeemed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> My tongue also shall talk of youri righteousness all the day long: for they are confounded, for they are brought unto shame, that seek my hurt.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; for Shlomo (Solomon) &#91;Peace&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Give the king youri judgments, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and youri righteousness unto the king’s son.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> He shall judge youri people with righteousness, and youri poor with judgment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> The mountains shall bring peace to the people, and the little hills, by righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> He shall judge the poor of the people, he shall save the children of the needy, and shall break in pieces the oppressor.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> They shall fear youi as long as the sun and moon endure, throughout all generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> He shall come down like rain upon the mown grass: as showers &#91;that&#93; water the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> In his days shall the righteous flourish; and abundance of peace so long as the moon endures.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> He shall have dominion also from sea to sea, and from the river unto the ends of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> They that dwell in the wilderness shall bow before him; and his enemies shall lick the dust.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> The kings of Tarshish &#91;Yellow Jasper&#93; and of the isles shall bring presents: the kings of Sheva &#91;Oath; Seven&#93; and S’va &#91;Drink Thou&#93; shall offer gifts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Yea, all kings shall fall down before him: all nations shall serve him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> For he shall deliver the needy when he cries; the poor also, and &#91;him&#93; that has no helper.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> He shall spare the poor and needy, and shall save the souls of the needy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> He shall redeem their soul from deceit and violence: and precious shall their blood be in his sight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And he shall live, and to him shall be given of the gold of Sheva &#91;Oath; Seven&#93;: prayer also shall be made for him continually; &#91;and&#93; daily shall he be praised.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> There shall be an handful of corn in the earth upon the top of the mountains; the fruit thereof shall shake like L’vanon &#91;White&#93;: and &#91;they&#93; of the city shall flourish like grass of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> His name shall endure forever: his name shall be continued as long as the sun: and &#91;men&#93; shall be Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) in him: all nations shall call him Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;, who only does wondrous things.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> And Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; his glorious name forever: and let the whole earth be filled &#91;with&#93; his glory; Amen, and Amen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> The prayers of David &#91;Beloved&#93; the son of Yishai &#91;I possess&#93; are ended.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of Asaph &#91;Gatherer&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Truly God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; good to Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;, &#91;even&#93; to such as are of a clean heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> But as for me, my feet were almost gone; my steps had well near slipped.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> For I was envious at the foolish, &#91;when&#93; I saw the prosperity of the wicked.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> For &#91;there are&#93; no bands in their death: but their strength &#91;is&#93; firm.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> They &#91;are&#93; not in trouble &#91;as other&#93; men; neither are they plagued like &#91;other&#93; men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Therefore pride compasses them about as a chain; violence covers them &#91;as&#93; a garment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Their eyes stand out with fatness: they have more than heart could wish.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> They are corrupt, and speak wickedly &#91;concerning&#93; oppression: they speak loftily.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> They set their mouth against the heavens, and their tongue walks through the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Therefore his people return here: and waters of a full &#91;cup&#93; are wrung out to them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> And they say, How does God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; know? and is there knowledge in the most High?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Behold, these &#91;are&#93; the ungodly, who prosper in the world; they increase &#91;in&#93; riches.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Truly I have cleansed my heart &#91;in&#93; vain, and washed my hands in innocency.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> For all the day long have I been plagued, and chastened every morning.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> If I say, I will speak thus; behold, I should offend (make angry; make one stumble; violate) &#91;against&#93; the generation of youri children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> When I thought to know this, it &#91;was&#93; too painful for me;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> Until I went into the sanctuary of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; &#91;then&#93; understood I their end.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Surely youi did set them in slippery places: youi cast them down into destruction.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> How are they &#91;brought&#93; into desolation, as in a moment! they are utterly consumed with terrors.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> As a dream when &#91;one&#93; awakes; &#91;so&#93;, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, when youi awake, youi shall despise their image.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Thus my heart was grieved, and I was pricked in my reins (literally, kidneys; figuratively, seat of emotion).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> So foolish &#91;was&#93; I, and ignorant: I was &#91;as&#93; a beast before youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Nevertheless I &#91;am&#93; continually with youi: youi have held &#91;me&#93; by my right hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> youi shall guide me with youri counsel, and afterward receive me &#91;to&#93; glory.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Whom have I in heaven &#91;but youi&#93;? and &#91;there is&#93; none upon earth &#91;that&#93; I desire beside youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> My flesh and my heart fails: &#91;but&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; the strength of my heart, and my portion forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> For, lo, they that are far from youi shall perish: youi have destroyed all them that go a whoring from youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> But &#91;it is&#93; good for me to draw near to God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: I have put my trust in the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; GOD-Yehōvih &#91;THE FATHER&#93;, that I may declare all youri works.<span className="desc">Maskhil of Asaph &#91;Gatherer&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, why have youi cast &#91;us&#93; off forever? &#91;why&#93; does youri anger smoke against the sheep of youri pasture?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Remember youri congregation, &#91;which&#93; youi have purchased of old; the rod of youri inheritance, &#91;which&#93; youi have redeemed; this mount Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;, wherein youi have dwelt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Lift up youri feet unto the perpetual desolations; &#91;even&#93; all &#91;that&#93; the enemy has done wickedly in the sanctuary.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Your2s enemies roar in the midst of youri congregations; they set up their ensigns &#91;for&#93; signs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> &#91;A man&#93; was famous according as he had lifted up axes upon the thick trees.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> But now they break down the carved work thereof at once with axes and hammers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> They have cast fire into youri sanctuary, they have defiled &#91;by casting down&#93; the dwelling place of youri name to the ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> They said in their hearts, Let us destroy them together: they have burned up all the synagogues of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in the land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> We see not our signs: &#91;there is&#93; no more any prophet: neither &#91;is there&#93; among us any that knows how long.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, how long shall the adversary reproach (disgrace; shame)? shall the enemy blaspheme youri name forever?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Why withdraw youi youri hand, even youri right hand? pluck &#91;it&#93; out of youri bosom.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> For God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; my King of old, working salvation in the midst of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> youi did divide the sea by youri strength: youi broke the heads of the dragons in the waters.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> youi broke the heads of leviathan in pieces, &#91;and&#93; gave him &#91;to be&#93; meat to the people inhabiting the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> youi did cleave (split open; cut open; divide) the fountain and the flood: youi dry up mighty rivers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> The day &#91;is&#93; yoursi, the night also &#91;is&#93; yoursi: youi have prepared the light and the sun.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> youi have set all the borders of the earth: youi have made summer and winter.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Remember this, &#91;that&#93; the enemy has reproached, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and &#91;that&#93; the foolish people have blasphemed youi name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> O deliver not the soul of youri turtledove unto the multitude &#91;of the wicked&#93;: forget not the congregation of youri poor forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Have respect unto the covenant: for the dark places of the earth are full of the habitations of cruelty.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> O let not the oppressed return ashamed: let the poor and needy praise youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Arise, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, plead youri own cause: remember how the foolish man reproaches youi daily.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Forget not the voice of youri enemies: the tumult of those that rise up against youi increases continually.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, “Do not Destroy,” A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) &#91;or&#93; Song of Asaph &#91;Gatherer&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Unto youi, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, do we give thanks, &#91;unto youi&#93; do we give thanks: for &#91;that&#93; youri name is near youri wondrous works declare.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> When I shall receive the congregation I will judge uprightly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> The earth and all the inhabitants thereof are dissolved: I bear up the pillars of it. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> I said unto the fools, Deal not foolishly: and to the wicked, Lift not up the horn:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Lift not up your horn on high: speak &#91;not with&#93; a stiff neck.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> For promotion &#91;cometh&#93; neither from the east, nor from the west, nor from the south.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> But God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; the judge: he puts down one, and sets up another.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> For in the hand of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;there is&#93; a cup, and the wine is red; it is full of mixture; and he pours out of the same: but the dregs (grounds at the bottom of a cup) thereof, all the wicked of the earth shall wring &#91;them&#93; out, &#91;and&#93; drink &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> But I will declare forever; I will sing praises to the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> All the horns of the wicked also will I cut off; &#91;but&#93; the horns of the righteous shall be exalted.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader on N’ginot &#91;stringed instruments&#93;, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) &#91;or&#93; Song of Asaph &#91;Gatherer&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> In Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; &#91;is&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; known: his name &#91;is&#93; great in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> In Salem also is his tabernacle, and his dwelling place in Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> There brake he the arrows of the bow, the shield, and the sword, and the battle. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> youi &#91;are&#93; more glorious &#91;and&#93; excellent than the mountains of prey.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> The stouthearted are spoiled, they have slept their sleep: and none of the men of might have found their hands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> At youri rebuke (reprimand; strongly warn; restrain), O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;, both the chariot and horse are cast into a dead sleep.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> youi, &#91;even&#93; youi, &#91;are&#93; to be feared: and who may stand in youri sight when once youi are angry?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> youi did cause judgment to be heard from heaven; the earth feared, and was still,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> When God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; arose to judgment, to save all the meek of the earth. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Surely the wrath of man shall praise youi: the remainder of wrath shall youi restrain.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Vow, and pay unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; your God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: let all that be round about him bring presents unto him that ought to be feared.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> He shall cut off the spirit of princes: &#91;he is&#93; terrible to the kings of the earth.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, to, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of Asaph &#91;Gatherer&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I cried unto God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; with my voice, &#91;even&#93; unto God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; with my voice; and he gave ear unto me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> In the day of my trouble I sought the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;: my sore ran in the night, and ceased not: my soul refused to be comforted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> I remembered God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and was troubled: I complained, and my spirit was overwhelmed. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> youi hold mine eyes waking: I am so troubled that I cannot speak.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> I have considered the days of old, the years of ancient times.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> I call to (to do entirely, do wholly) remembrance my song in the night: I commune with mine own heart: and my spirit made diligent search.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Will the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; cast off forever? and will he be favourable no more?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Is his mercy clean gone forever? does &#91;his&#93; promise fail forevermore?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Hath God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; forgotten to be gracious? has he in anger shut up his tender mercies? Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> And I said, This &#91;is&#93; my infirmity: &#91;but I will remember&#93; the years of the right hand of the most High.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> I will remember the works of the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): surely I will remember youri wonders of old.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> I will meditate also of all youri work, and talk of youri doings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Your2s way, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;is&#93; in the sanctuary: who &#91;is so&#93; great a God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; as &#91;our&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> youi &#91;are&#93; the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; that do wonders: youi have declared youri strength among the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> youi have with &#91;youri&#93; arm redeemed youri people, the sons of Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93; and Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> The waters saw youi, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the waters saw youi; they were afraid: the depths also were troubled.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> The clouds poured out water: the skies sent out a sound: youri arrows also went abroad.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> The voice of youri thunder &#91;was&#93; in the heaven: the lightnings lightened the world: the earth trembled and shook.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Your2s way &#91;is&#93; in the sea, and youri path in the great waters, and youri footsteps are not known.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> youi led youri people like a flock by the hand of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; and Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;.<span className="desc">Maskhil &#91;Contemplative poem&#93; of Asaph &#91;Gatherer&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Give ear, O my people, &#91;to&#93; my law: incline your ears to the words of my mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> I will open my mouth in a parable: I will utter dark sayings of old:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Which we have heard and known, and our fathers have told us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> We will not hide &#91;them&#93; from their children, showing to the generation to come the praises of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and his strength, and his wonderful works that he has done.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> For he established a testimony in Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;, and appointed a law in Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;, which he commanded our fathers, that they should make them known to their children:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> That the generation to come might know &#91;them, even&#93; the children &#91;which&#93; should be born; &#91;who&#93; should arise and declare &#91;them&#93; to their children:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> That they might set their hope in God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and not forget the works of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, but keep his commandments:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> And might not be as their fathers, a stubborn and rebellious generation; a generation &#91;that&#93; set not their heart aright (in the right way), and whose spirit was not steadfast with God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> The children of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;, &#91;being&#93; armed, &#91;and&#93; carrying bows, turned back in the day of battle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> They kept not the covenant of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and refused to walk in his law;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> And forgot his works, and his wonders that he had showed them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Marvelous things did he in the sight of their fathers, in the land of Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, &#91;in&#93; the field of Tzo’an &#91;Place of Departure&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> He divided the sea, and caused them to pass through; and he made the waters to stand as an heap.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> In the daytime also he led them with a cloud, and all the night with a light of fire.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> He clave the rocks in the wilderness, and gave &#91;them&#93; drink as &#91;out of&#93; the great depths.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> He brought streams also out of the rock, and caused waters to run down like rivers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> And they sinned yet more against him by provoking the most High in the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> And they tempted God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in their heart by asking meat for their lust.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Yea, they spoke against God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; they said, Can God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; furnish a table in the wilderness?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Behold, he smote the rock, that the waters gushed out, and the streams overflowed; can he give bread also? can he provide flesh for his people?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Therefore the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; heard &#91;this&#93;, and was exceedingly angry: so a fire was kindled against Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;, and anger also came up against Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Because they believed not in God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and trusted not in his salvation:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Though he had commanded the clouds from above, and opened the doors of heaven,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And had rained down manna upon them to eat, and had given them of the corn of heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Man did eat angels’ food: he sent them meat to the full.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> He caused an east wind to blow in the heaven: and by his power he brought in the south wind.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> He rained flesh also upon them as dust, and feathered fowls like as the sand of the sea:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> And he let &#91;it&#93; fall in the midst of their camp, round about their habitations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> So they did eat, and were well filled: for he gave them their own desire;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> They were not estranged from their lust. But while their meat &#91;was&#93; yet in their mouths,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> The wrath of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; came upon them, and slew the fattest of them, and smote down the chosen &#91;men&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> For all this they sinned still, and believed not for his wondrous works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Therefore their days did he consume in vanity, and their years in trouble.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> When he slew them, then they sought him: and they returned and enquired early after God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And they remembered that God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;was&#93; their rock, and the high God-El ‘Elyon &#91;The Most High Powerful Living Word&#93; their redeemer.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> Nevertheless they did flatter him with their mouth, and they lied unto him with their tongues.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> For their heart was not right with him, neither were they steadfast in his covenant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> But he, &#91;being&#93; full of compassion, forgave &#91;their&#93; iniquity, and destroyed &#91;them&#93; not: yea, many a time turned he his anger away, and did not stir up all his wrath.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> For he remembered that they &#91;were but&#93; flesh; a wind that passes away, and cometh not again.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> How oft did they provoke him in the wilderness, &#91;and&#93; grieve him in the desert!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> Yea, they turned back and tempted God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and limited the Holy One of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> They remembered not his hand, &#91;nor&#93; the day when he delivered them from the enemy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> How he had wrought his signs in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;, and his wonders in the field of Tzo’an &#91;Place of Departure&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And had turned their rivers into blood; and their floods, that they could not drink.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> He sent diverse sorts of flies among them, which devoured them; and frogs, which destroyed them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> He gave also their increase unto the caterpillar, and their labour unto the locust.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> He destroyed their vines with hail, and their sycamore trees with frost.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> He gave up their cattle also to the hail, and their flocks to hot thunderbolts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> He cast upon them the fierceness of his anger, wrath, and indignation, and trouble, by sending evil angels &#91;among them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> He made a way to his anger; he spared not their soul from death, but gave their life over to the pestilence;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> And smote all the firstborn in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; the chief of &#91;their&#93; strength in the tabernacles of Ham:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> But made his own people to go forth like sheep, and guided them in the wilderness like a flock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> And he led them on safely, so that they feared not: but the sea overwhelmed their enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54"> And he brought them to the border of his sanctuary, &#91;even to&#93; this mountain, &#91;which&#93; his right hand had purchased.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> He cast out the heathen also before them, and divided them an inheritance by line, and made the tribes of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; to dwell in their tents.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> Yet they tempted and provoked the most high God-El ‘Elyon &#91;The Most High Powerful Living Word&#93;, and kept not his testimonies:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57"> But turned back, and dealt unfaithfully like their fathers: they were turned aside like a deceitful bow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v58" data-v="58"> For they provoked him to anger with their high places, and moved him to jealousy with their graven images.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v59" data-v="59"> When God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; heard &#91;this&#93;, he was exceedingly angry, and greatly abhorred Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v60" data-v="60"> So that he forsook the tabernacle of Shiloh &#91;Peace; Rest&#93;, the tent &#91;which&#93; he placed among men;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v61" data-v="61"> And delivered his strength into captivity, and his glory into the enemy’s hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v62" data-v="62"> He gave his people over also unto the sword; and was exceedingly angry with his inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v63" data-v="63"> The fire consumed their young men; and their maidens were not given to marriage.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v64" data-v="64"> Their priests fell by the sword; and their widows made no lamentation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v65" data-v="65"> Then the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; awaked as one out of sleep, &#91;and&#93; like a mighty man that shouts by reason of wine.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v66" data-v="66"> And he smote his enemies in the hinder parts: he put them to a perpetual reproach (disgrace; shame).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v67" data-v="67"> Moreover he refused the tabernacle of Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, and chose not the tribe of Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v68" data-v="68"> But chose the tribe of Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93;, the mount Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93; which he loved.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v69" data-v="69"> And he built his sanctuary like high &#91;palaces&#93;, like the earth which he has established forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v70" data-v="70"> He chose David &#91;Beloved&#93; also his servant, and took him from the sheepfolds:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v71" data-v="71"> From following the ewes great with young he brought him to feed Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93; his people, and Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; his inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v72" data-v="72"> So he fed them according to the integrity of his heart; and guided them by the skillfulness of his hands.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of Asaph &#91;Gatherer&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the heathen are come into youri inheritance; youri holy temple have they defiled; they have laid Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93; on heaps.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> The dead bodies of youri servants have they given &#91;to be&#93; meat unto the fowls of the heaven, the flesh of youri saints unto the beasts of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Their blood have they shed like water round about Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;; and &#91;there was&#93; none to bury &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> We are become a reproach (disgrace; shame) to our neighbours, a scorn and ridicule (laughing at enemies’ threats) to them that are round about us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> How long, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;? will youi be angry forever? shall youri jealousy burn like fire?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Pour out youri wrath upon the heathen that have not known youi, and upon the kingdoms that have not called upon youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> For they have devoured Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;, and laid waste his dwelling place.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> O remember not against us former iniquities: let youri tender mercies speedily prevent (come before; go before; anticipate) us: for we are brought very low.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Help us, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of our salvation, for the glory of youri name: and deliver us, and purge away our sins, for youri name’s sake.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> For what reason should the heathen say, Where &#91;is&#93; their God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;? let him be known among the heathen in our sight &#91;by&#93; the revenging of the blood of youri servants &#91;which is&#93; shed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Let the sighing of the prisoner come before youi; according to the greatness of youri power preserve youi those that are appointed to die;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> And render unto our neighbours sevenfold into their bosom their reproach (disgrace; shame), wherewith they have reproached youi, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> So we youri people and sheep of youri pasture will give youi thanks forever: we will show forth youri praise to all generations.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader set to “Lilies,” A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of Asaph &#91;Gatherer&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Give ear, O Shepherd of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;, youi that leads Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93; like a flock; youi that dwell &#91;between&#93; the k’ruvim (Cherubim), shine forth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Before Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; and Binyamin &#91;Son of the Right Hand/Strength&#93; and M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; stir up youri strength, and come &#91;and&#93; save us.</span>
</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Turn us again, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, and cause youri face to shine; and we shall be saved.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> For youi, Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, &#91;are&#93; good, and ready to forgive; and plenteous in mercy unto all them that call upon youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> In the day of my trouble I will call upon youi: for youi will answer me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Among the gods &#91;there is&#93; none like unto youi, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;; neither &#91;are there any works&#93; like unto youri works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> All nations whom youi have made shall come and worship before youi, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;; and shall glorify youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> For youi &#91;are&#93; great, and do wondrous things: youi &#91;are&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; alone.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Teach me youri way, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; I will walk in youri truth: unite my heart to fear youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> I will praise youi, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; my God-Elohai &#91;GOD&#93;, with all my heart: and I will glorify youri name forevermore.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> For great &#91;is&#93; youri mercy toward me: and youi have delivered my soul from the lowest hell.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, the proud are risen against me, and the assemblies of violent &#91;men&#93; have sought after my soul; and have not set youi before them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> But youi, O Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, &#91;are&#93; a God-El &#91;a God&#93; full of compassion, and gracious, longsuffering, and plenteous in mercy and truth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> O turn unto me, and have mercy upon me; give youri strength unto youri servant, and save the son of youri handmaid.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> Show me a token for good; that they which hate me may see &#91;it&#93;, and be ashamed: because youi, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, have given hope to me, and comforted me.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) &#91;or&#93; Song for the sons of Korakh &#91;Bald&#93;.</span></span>

<div className="bp">

Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O LORD-Yehōvah God-Elohe Yeshuati &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate, The Powerful Living Word of My Salvation&#93;, I have cried day &#91;and&#93; night before youi:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Your2s wrath lies hard upon me, and youi have afflicted &#91;me&#93; with all youri waves. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> youi have put away mine acquaintance far from me; youi have made me an abomination unto them: &#91;I am&#93; shut up, and I cannot come forth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Mine eye mourns by reason of affliction: LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, I have called daily upon youi, I have stretched out my hands unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Will youi show wonders to the dead? shall the dead arise &#91;and&#93; praise youi? Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Shall youri lovingkindness be declared in the grave? &#91;or&#93; youri faithfulness in destruction?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Shall youri wonders be known in the dark? and youri righteousness in the land of forgetfulness?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> But unto youi have I cried, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and in the morning shall my prayer prevent (come before; go before; anticipate) youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, why cast youi off my soul? &#91;why&#93; hide youi youri face from me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> I &#91;am&#93; afflicted and ready to die from &#91;my&#93; youth up: &#91;while&#93; I suffer youri terrors I am distracted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> For who in the heaven can be compared unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;? &#91;who&#93; among the sons of the mighty can be likened unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; is greatly to be feared in the assembly of the saints, and to be had in reverence of all &#91;them that are&#93; about him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; Tz’va’ot &#91;of the Heavenly Armies&#93;, who &#91;is&#93; a strong LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) like unto youi? or to youri faithfulness round about youi?</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> youi rule the raging of the sea: when the waves thereof arise, youi stillest them.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> The heavens &#91;are&#93; yoursi, the earth also &#91;is&#93; yoursi: &#91;as for&#93; the world and the fullness thereof, youi have founded them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> The north and the south youi have created them: Tavor &#91;Mound&#93; and Hermon &#91;A Sanctuary&#93; shall rejoice in youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> youi have a mighty arm: strong is youri hand, &#91;and&#93; high is youri right hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Justice and judgment &#91;are&#93; the habitation of youri throne: mercy and tshall go before youri face.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;is&#93; the people that know the joyful sound: they shall walk, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, in the light of youri Countenance (Face, appearance; show favor).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> In youri name shall they rejoice all the day: and in youri righteousness shall they be exalted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> For youi &#91;are&#93; the glory of their strength: and in youri favour our horn shall be exalted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> For the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; our defense; and the Holy One of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; &#91;is&#93; our king.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Then youi spoke in vision to youri holy one, and said, I have laid help upon &#91;one that is&#93; mighty; I have exalted &#91;one&#93; chosen out of the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> I have found David &#91;Beloved&#93; my servant; with my holy oil have I anointed him:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> With whom my hand shall be established: mine arm also shall strengthen him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> The enemy shall not exact upon him; nor the son of wickedness afflict him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> And I will beat down his foes before his face, and plague them that hate him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> But my faithfulness and my mercy &#91;shall be&#93; with him: and in my name shall his horn be exalted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> I will set his hand also in the sea, and his right hand in the rivers.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> He shall cry unto me, youi &#91;are&#93; my father, my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and the Rock of my Salvation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Also I will make him &#91;my&#93; firstborn, higher than the kings of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> My mercy will I keep for him forevermore, and my covenant shall stand fast with him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> His seed also will I make &#91;to endure&#93; forever, and his throne as the days of heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> If his children forsake my law, and walk not in my judgments;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> If they break my statutes, and keep not my commandments;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Then will I visit their transgression with the rod, and their iniquity with stripes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Nevertheless my lovingkindness will I not utterly take from him, nor suffer my faithfulness to fail.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> My covenant will I not break, nor alter the thing that is gone out of my lips.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Once have I sworn by my holiness that I will not lie unto David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> His seed shall endure forever, and his throne as the sun before me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> It shall be established forever as the moon, and &#91;as&#93; a faithful witness in heaven. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> But youi have cast off and abhorred, youi have been exceedingly angry with youri anointed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> youi have made void the covenant of youri servant: youi have profaned his crown &#91;by casting it&#93; to the ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> youi have broken down all his hedges; youi have brought his strong holds to ruin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> All that pass by the way spoil (plunder; prey; booty) him: he is a reproach (disgrace; shame) to his neighbours.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> youi have set up the right hand of his adversaries; youi have made all his enemies to rejoice.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> youi have also turned the edge of his sword, and have not made him to stand in the battle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> youi have made his glory to cease, and cast his throne down to the ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> The days of his youth have youi shortened: youi have covered him with shame. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> How long, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;? will youi hide yourself forever? shall youri wrath burn like fire?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> Remember how short my time is: wherefore have youi made all men in vain?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> What man &#91;is he that&#93; lives, and shall not see death? shall he deliver his soul from the hand of the grave? Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, where &#91;are&#93; youri former lovingkindnesses, &#91;which&#93; youi swore unto David &#91;Beloved&#93; in youri truth?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> Remember, Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, the reproach (disgrace; shame) of youri servants; &#91;how&#93; I do bear in my bosom &#91;the reproach (disgrace; shame) of&#93; all the mighty people;</span>
</div>

<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> Wherewith youri enemies have reproached, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; wherewith they have reproached the footsteps of youri anointed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

Amen, and Amen.<span className="desc">A Prayer of Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; the man of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span></span>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, youi have been our dwelling place in all generations.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Before the mountains were brought forth, or ever youi had formed the earth and the world, even from everlasting to everlasting, youi &#91;are&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>

</div>

<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> In the morning it flourishes, and grows up; in the evening it is cut down, and withers.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> For we are consumed by youri anger, and by youri wrath are we troubled.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> youi have set our iniquities before youi, our secret &#91;sins&#93; in the light of youri Countenance (Face, appearance; show favor).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> For all our days are passed away in youri wrath: we spend our years as a tale &#91;that is told&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> The days of our years &#91;are&#93; threescore years and ten; and if by reason of strength &#91;they be&#93; fourscore years, yet &#91;is&#93; their strength labour and sorrow; for it is soon cut off, and we fly away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Who knows the power of youri anger? even according to youri fear, &#91;so is&#93; youri wrath.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Return, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, how long? and let it repent youi concerning youri servants.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Make us glad according to the days &#91;wherein&#93; youi have afflicted us, &#91;and&#93; the years &#91;wherein&#93; we have seen evil.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Let youri work appear unto youri servants, and youri glory unto their children.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> And let the beauty of the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; —our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; —be upon us: and establish youi the work of our hands upon us; yea, the work of our hands establish youi it.</span>

</div>

</div>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Yet they say, The LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) shall not see, neither shall the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93; regard &#91;it&#93;.</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> For I have eaten ashes like bread, and mingled my drink with weeping,</span>

</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> He made known his ways unto Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93;, his acts unto the people of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; merciful and gracious, slow to anger, and plenteous in mercy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> He will not always quarrel: neither will he keep &#91;his anger&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> He has not dealt with us after our sins; nor rewarded us according to our iniquities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> For as the heaven is high above the earth, &#91;so&#93; great is his mercy toward them that fear him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> As far as the east is from the west, &#91;so&#93; far has he removed our transgressions from us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Like as a father pities &#91;his&#93; children, &#91;so&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; pities them that fear him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> For he knows our frame; he remembers that we &#91;are&#93; dust.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> &#91;As for&#93; man, his days &#91;are&#93; as grass: as a flower of the field, so he flourishes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> For the wind passes over it, and it is gone; and the place thereof shall know it no more.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> But the mercy of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; from everlasting to everlasting upon them that fear him, and his righteousness unto children’s children;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> To such as keep his covenant, and to those that remember his commandments to do them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; has prepared his throne in the heavens; and his kingdom rules over all.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, you2f his angels, that excel in strength, that do his commandments, hearkening unto the voice of his word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Bless you2f the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, all &#91;you2f&#93; his hosts; &#91;you2f&#93; ministers of his, that do his pleasure.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, all his works in all places of his dominion: bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, O my soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, O my soul. O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, youi are very great; youi are clothed with honour and majesty.</span>
