<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Who cover &#91;yourself&#93; with light as &#91;with&#93; a garment: who stretches out the heavens like a curtain:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Who lays the beams of his chambers in the waters: who makes the clouds his chariot: who walks upon the wings of the wind:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Who makes his angels spirits; his ministers a flaming fire:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> &#91;Who&#93; laid the foundations of the earth, &#91;that&#93; it should not be removed forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> youi covered it with the deep as &#91;with&#93; a garment: the waters stood above the mountains.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> At youri rebuke (reprimand; strongly warn; restrain) they fled; at the voice of youri thunder they hasted away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> They go up by the mountains; they go down by the valleys unto the place which youi have founded for them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> youi have set a bound that they may not pass over; that they turn not again to cover the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> He send the springs into the valleys, &#91;which&#93; run among the hills.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> They give drink to every beast of the field: the wild asses quench their thirst.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> By them shall the fowls of the heaven have their habitation, &#91;which&#93; sing among the branches.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> He waters the hills from his chambers: the earth is satisfied with the fruit of youri works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> He causes the grass to grow for the cattle, and herb for the service of man: that he may bring forth food out of the earth;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And wine &#91;that&#93; makes glad the heart of man, &#91;and&#93; oil to make &#91;his&#93; face to shine, and bread &#91;which&#93; strengthens man’s heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> The trees of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; are full &#91;of sap&#93;; the cedars of L’vanon &#91;White&#93;, which he has planted;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> Where the birds make their nests: &#91;as for&#93; the stork, the fir trees &#91;are&#93; her house.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> The high hills &#91;are&#93; a refuge for the wild goats; &#91;and&#93; the rocks for the conies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> He appointed the moon for seasons: the sun knows his going down.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> youi make darkness, and it is night: wherein all the beasts of the forest do creep &#91;forth&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> The young lions roar after their prey, and seek their meat from God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> The sun arises, they gather themselves together, and lay them down in their dens.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Man goes forth unto his work and to his labour until the evening.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, how manifold are youri works! in wisdom have youi made them all: the earth is full of youri riches.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> &#91;So is&#93; this great and wide sea, wherein &#91;are&#93; things creeping innumerable, both small and great beasts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> There go the ships: &#91;there is&#93; that leviathan, &#91;whom&#93; youi have made to play therein.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> These wait all upon youi; that youi may give &#91;them&#93; their meat in due season.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> &#91;That&#93; youi give them they gather: youi open youri hand, they are filled with good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> youi hide youri face, they are troubled: youi take away their breath, they die, and return to their dust.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> youi send forth youri Spirit, they are created: and youi renew the face of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> The glory of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall endure forever: the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall rejoice in his works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> He looks on the earth, and it trembles: he touches the hills, and they smoke.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> I will sing unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; as long as I live: I will sing praise to my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; while I have my being.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> My meditation of him shall be sweet: I will be glad in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Let the sinners be consumed out of the earth, and let the wicked be no more. Bless youi the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, O my soul. Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O give thanks unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; call upon his name: make known his deeds among the people.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Sing unto him, sing Tehillim &#91;Songs of Praise&#93; unto him: talk you2f of all his wondrous works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Glory you2f in his holy name: let the heart of them rejoice that seek the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Seek the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and his strength: seek his face evermore.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Remember his marvelous works that he has done; his wonders, and the judgments of his mouth;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> O you2f seed of Abraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93; his servant, you2f children of Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93; his chosen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> He &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: his judgments &#91;are&#93; in all the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> He has remembered his covenant forever, the word &#91;which&#93; he commanded to a thousand generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Which &#91;covenant&#93; he made with Abraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93;, and his oath unto Yitz’khak &#91;Laughter&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> And confirmed the same unto Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93; for a law, &#91;and&#93; to Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; &#91;for&#93; an everlasting covenant:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Saying, Unto youi will I give the land of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;, the Lot &#91;Covering&#93; of your inheritance:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> When they were &#91;but&#93; a few men in number; yea, very few, and strangers in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> When they went from one nation to another, from &#91;one&#93; kingdom to another people;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> He suffered no man to do them wrong: yea, he reproved kings for their sakes;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> &#91;Saying&#93;, Touch not mine anointed, and do my prophets no harm.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Moreover he called for a famine upon the land: he brake the whole staff of bread.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> He sent a man before them, &#91;even&#93; Yosef &#91;(God) He Shall Add&#93;, &#91;who&#93; was sold for a servant:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Whose feet they hurt with fetters: he was laid in Yir’on &#91;fearing&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Until the time that his word came: the word of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; tried him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> The king sent and loosed him; &#91;even&#93; the ruler of the people, and let him go free.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> He made him lord of his house, and ruler of all his substance:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> To bind his princes at his pleasure; and teach his senators wisdom.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; also came into Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; and Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93; sojourned in the land of Ham.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And he increased his people greatly; and made them stronger than their enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> He turned their heart to hate his people, to deal subtly with his servants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> He sent Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; his servant; &#91;and&#93; Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; whom he had chosen.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> They showed his signs among them, and wonders in the land of Ham.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> He sent darkness, and made it dark; and they rebelled not against his word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> He turned their waters into blood, and slew their fish.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Their land brought forth frogs in abundance, in the chambers of their kings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> He spoke, and there came diverse sorts of flies, &#91;and&#93; lice in all their coasts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> He gave them hail for rain, &#91;and&#93; flaming fire in their land.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> He smote their vines also and their fig trees; and brake the trees of their coasts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> He spoke, and the locusts came, and caterpillars, and that without number,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> And did eat up all the herbs in their land, and devoured the fruit of their ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> He smote also all the firstborn in their land, the chief of all their strength.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> He brought them forth also with silver and gold: and &#91;there was&#93; not one feeble &#91;person&#93; among their tribes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; was glad when they departed: for the fear of them fell upon them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> He spread a cloud for a covering; and fire to give light in the night.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> &#91;The people&#93; asked, and he brought quails, and satisfied them with the bread of heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> He opened the rock, and the waters gushed out; they ran in the dry places &#91;like&#93; a river.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> For he remembered his holy promise, &#91;and&#93; Abraham &#91;Exalted Father&#93; his servant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And he brought forth his people with joy, &#91;and&#93; his chosen with gladness:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> And gave them the lands of the heathen: and they inherited the labour of the people;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> That they might observe his statutes, and keep his laws. Praise you2f the LORD-Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Praise you2f the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;. O give thanks unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; for &#91;he is&#93; good: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Who can utter the mighty acts of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;? &#91;who&#93; can show forth all his praise?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;are&#93; they that keep judgment, &#91;and&#93; he that does righteousness at all times.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Remember me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, with the favour &#91;that youi bare unto&#93; youri people: O visit me with youri salvation;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> That I may see the good of youri chosen, that I may rejoice in the gladness of youri nation, that I may glory with youri inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> We have sinned with our fathers, we have committed iniquity, we have done wickedly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Our fathers understood not youri wonders in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;; they remembered not the multitude of youri mercies; but provoked &#91;him&#93; at the sea, &#91;even&#93; at the Red sea.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Nevertheless he saved them for his name’s sake, that he might make his mighty power to be known.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> He rebuked the Red sea also, and it was dried up: so he led them through the depths, as through the wilderness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> And he saved them from the hand of him that hated &#91;them&#93;, and redeemed them from the hand of the enemy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> And the waters covered their enemies: there was not one of them left.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Then believed they his words; they sang his praise.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> They soon forgot his works; they waited not for his counsel:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> But lusted exceedingly in the wilderness, and tempted God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; in the desert.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> And he gave them their request; but sent leanness into their soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> They envied Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; also in the camp, &#91;and&#93; Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93; the saint of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> The earth opened and swallow (to approve, commend, accept)ed up Datan &#91;belonging to a fountain&#93;, and covered the company of Aviram &#91;My Father is Exalted&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> And a fire was kindled in their company; the flame burned up the wicked.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> They made a calf in Horev &#91;Desert&#93;, and worshipped the molten image.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Thus they changed their glory into the similitude of an ox that eats grass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> They forgot God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; their saviour, which had done great things in Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Wondrous works in the land of Ham, &#91;and&#93; terrible things by the Red sea.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Therefore he said that he would destroy them, had not Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; his chosen stood before him in the breach (breaking forth; a break; opening, inlet), to turn away his wrath, lest he should destroy &#91;them&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Yea, they despised the pleasant land, they believed not his word:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> But murmured in their tents, &#91;and&#93; hearkened not unto the voice of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Therefore he lifted up his hand against them, to overthrow them in the wilderness:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> To overthrow their seed also among the nations, and to scatter them in the lands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> They joined themselves also unto Ba’al-P’or &#91;Lord of the Opening&#93;, and ate the sacrifices of the dead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Thus they provoked &#91;him&#93; to anger with their inventions: and the plague brake in upon them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Then stood up Pinchas &#91;Turn/Hide&#93;, and executed judgment: and &#91;so&#93; the plague was detained (held).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> And that was counted unto him for righteousness unto all generations forevermore.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> They angered &#91;him&#93; also at the waters of strife, so that it went ill with Moshe &#91;He Who Draws Out Of The Waters&#93; for their sakes:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> Because they provoked his spirit, so that he spoke unadvisedly with his lips.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> They did not destroy the nations, concerning whom the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; commanded them:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> But were mingled among the heathen, and learned their works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And they served their idols: which were a snare unto them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Yea, they sacrificed their sons and their daughters unto devils,</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> And shed innocent blood, &#91;even&#93; the blood of their sons and of their daughters, whom they sacrificed unto the idols of Kena’an &#91;Merchant&#93;: and the land was polluted with blood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> Thus were they defiled with their own works, and went a whoring with their own inventions.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> Therefore was the wrath of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; kindled against his people, insomuch that he abhorred his own inheritance.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> And he gave them into the hand of the heathen; and they that hated them ruled over them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> Their enemies also oppressed them, and they were brought into subjection under their hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> Many times did he deliver them; but they provoked &#91;him&#93; with their counsel, and were brought low for their iniquity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> Nevertheless he regarded their affliction, when he heard their cry:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> And he remembered for them his covenant, and repented according to the multitude of his mercies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> He made them also to be pitied of all those that carried them captives.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> Save us, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and gather us from among the heathen, to give thanks unto youri holy name, &#91;and&#93; to triumph in youri praise.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; from everlasting to everlasting: and let all the people say, Amen. Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O give thanks unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, for &#91;he is&#93; good: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Let the redeemed of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; say &#91;so&#93;, whom he has redeemed from the hand of the enemy;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> And gathered them out of the lands, from the east, and from the west, from the north, and from the south.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> They wandered in the wilderness in a solitary way; they found no city to dwell in.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Hungry and thirsty, their soul fainted in them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Then they cried unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; in their trouble, &#91;and&#93; he delivered them out of their distresses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> And he led them forth by the right way, that they might go to a city of habitation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Oh that &#91;men&#93; would praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;for&#93; his goodness, and &#91;for&#93; his wonderful works to the children of men!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> For he satisfies the longing soul, and fills the hungry soul with goodness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Such as sit in darkness and in the shadow of death, &#91;being&#93; bound in affliction and Yir’on &#91;fearing&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Because they rebelled against the words of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and contemned the counsel of the most High:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Therefore he brought down their heart with labour; they fell down, and &#91;there was&#93; none to help.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Then they cried unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; in their trouble, &#91;and&#93; he saved them out of their distresses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> He brought them out of darkness and the shadow of death, and brake their bands in sunder.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Oh that &#91;men&#93; would praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;for&#93; his goodness, and &#91;for&#93; his wonderful works to the children of men!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> For he has broken the gates of brass, and cut the bars of Yir’on &#91;fearing&#93; in sunder.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> Fools because of their transgression, and because of their iniquities, are afflicted.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Their soul abhors all manner of meat; and they draw near unto the gates of death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Then they cry unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; in their trouble, &#91;and&#93; he saves them out of their distresses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> He sent his word, and healed them, and delivered &#91;them&#93; from their destructions.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Oh that &#91;men&#93; would praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;for&#93; his goodness, and &#91;for&#93; his wonderful works to the children of men!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> And let them sacrifice the sacrifices of thanksgiving, and declare his works with rejoicing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> They that go down to the sea in ships, that do business in great waters;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> These see the works of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and his wonders in the deep.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> For he commands, and raises the stormy wind, which lifts up the waves thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> They mount up to the heaven, they go down again to the depths: their soul is melted because of trouble.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> They reel (stagger, walk to and fro like a drunkard) to and fro, and stagger like a drunken man, and are at their wits’ end.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Then they cry unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; in their trouble, and he brings them out of their distresses.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> He makes the storm a calm, so that the waves thereof are still.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> Then are they glad because they be quiet; so he brings them unto their desired haven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> Oh that &#91;men&#93; would praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;for&#93; his goodness, and &#91;for&#93; his wonderful works to the children of men!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> Let them exalt him also in the congregation of the people, and praise him in the assembly of the elders.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> He turns rivers into a wilderness, and the watersprings into dry ground;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> A fruitful land into barrenness, for the wickedness of them that dwell therein.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> He turns the wilderness into a standing water, and dry ground into watersprings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> And there he makes the hungry to dwell, that they may prepare a city for habitation;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> And sow the fields, and plant vineyards, which may yield fruits of increase.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> He blesses them also, so that they are multiplied greatly; and suffers not their cattle to decrease.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> Again, they are diminished and brought low through oppression, affliction, and sorrow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> He pours contempt upon princes, and causes them to wander in the wilderness, &#91;where there is&#93; no way.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> Yet sets he the poor on high from affliction, and makes &#91;him&#93; families like a flock.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> The righteous shall see &#91;it&#93;, and rejoice: and all iniquity shall stop her mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> Whoso &#91;is&#93; wise, and will observe these &#91;things&#93;, even they shall understand the lovingkindness of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.<span className="desc">A Song &#91;or&#93; Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, my heart is fixed; I will sing and give praise, even with my glory.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Awake, Psaltery (stringed instrument, like an harp) and harp: I &#91;myself&#93; will awake early.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> I will praise youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, among the people: and I will sing praises unto youi among the nations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> For youri mercy &#91;is&#93; great above the heavens: and youri truth &#91;reaches&#93; unto the clouds.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Be youi exalted, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, above the heavens: and youri glory above all the earth;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> That youri beloved may be delivered: save &#91;with&#93; youri right hand, and answer me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; has spoken in his holiness; I will rejoice, I will divide Sh’khem &#91;Shoulder&#93;, and measure (deal out) out the valley of Sukkot &#91;Booths&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Gil’ad &#91;Rocky Region&#93; &#91;is&#93; mine; M’nasheh &#91;Causing to Forget&#93; &#91;is&#93; mine; Efrayim &#91;Doubly Fruitful&#93; also &#91;is&#93; the strength of mine head; Y’hudah &#91;Let Him (God) Be Praised&#93; &#91;is&#93; my lawgiver;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Mo’av &#91;Seed of my Father&#93; &#91;is&#93; my washpot; over Edom &#91;Red&#93; will I cast out my shoe; over Philistia will I triumph.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Who will bring me into the strong city? who will lead me into Edom &#91;Red&#93;?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> &#91;Will&#93; not &#91;youi&#93;, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, &#91;who&#93; have cast us off? and will not youi, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, go forth with our hosts?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Give us help from trouble: for vain &#91;is&#93; the help of man.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Through God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; we shall do valiantly: for he &#91;it is that&#93; shall tread down our enemies.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Hold not youri peace, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of my praise;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> For the mouth of the wicked and the mouth of the deceitful are opened against me: they have spoken against me with a lying tongue.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> They compassed me about also with words of hatred; and fought against me without a cause.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> For my love they are my adversaries: but I &#91;give myself unto&#93; prayer.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> And they have rewarded me evil for good, and hatred for my love.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Set youi a wicked man over him: and let Satan stand at his right hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> When he shall be judged, let him be condemned: and let his prayer become sin.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Let his days be few; &#91;and&#93; let another take his office.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Let his children be fatherless, and his wife a widow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Let his children be continually vagabonds, and beg: let them seek &#91;their bread&#93; also out of their desolate places.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Let the extortioner catch all that he has; and let the strangers spoil (plunder; prey; booty) his labour.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Let there be none to extend mercy unto him: neither let there be any to favour his fatherless children.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Let his posterity be cut off; &#91;and&#93; in the generation following let their name be blotted out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> Let the iniquity of his fathers be remembered with the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and let not the sin of his mother be blotted out.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Let them be before the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; continually, that he may cut off the memory of them from the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Because that he remembered not to show mercy, but persecuted the poor and needy man, that he might even slay the broken in heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> As he loved cursing, so let it come unto him: as he delighted not in blessing, so let it be far from him.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> As he clothed himself with cursing like as with his garment, so let it come into his bowels (inward parts; affections) like water, and like oil into his bones.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Let it be unto him as the garment &#91;which&#93; covers him, and for a belt (wrapped around) wherewith he is girded continually.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> &#91;Let&#93; this &#91;be&#93; the reward of mine adversaries from the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and of them that speak evil against my soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> But do youi for me, O GOD &#91;THE FATHER&#93; the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, for youri name’s sake: because youri mercy &#91;is&#93; good, deliver youi me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> For I &#91;am&#93; poor and needy, and my heart is wounded within me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> I am gone like the shadow when it declines: I am tossed up and down as the locust.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> My knees are weak through fasting; and my flesh fails of fatness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> I became also a reproach (disgrace; shame) unto them: &#91;when&#93; they looked upon me they shaked their heads.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Help me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: O save me according to youri mercy:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> That they may know that this &#91;is&#93; youri hand; &#91;that&#93; youi, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, have done it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> Let them curse, but bless youi: when they arise, let them be ashamed; but let youri servant rejoice.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Let mine adversaries be clothed with shame, and let them cover themselves with their own confusion, as with a Mantle (cloak; covering garment).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> I will greatly praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; with my mouth; yea, I will praise him among the multitude.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> For he shall stand at the right hand of the poor, to save &#91;him&#93; from those that condemn his soul.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; said unto my Lord &#91;Adon – Lord of the Earth&#93;, Sit youi at my right hand, until I make youri enemies youri footstool.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall send the rod of youri strength out of Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;: rule youi in the midst of youri enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Your2s people &#91;shall be&#93; willing in the day of youri power, in the beauties of holiness from the womb of the morning: youi have the dew of youri youth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; has sworn, and will not repent, youi &#91;are&#93; a priest forever after the order of Melkhi-Zedek (my king (is) righteous(ness)).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> The Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93; at youi right hand shall strike through kings in the day of his wrath.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> He shall judge among the heathen, he shall fill &#91;the places&#93; with the dead bodies; he shall wound the heads over many countries.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> He shall drink of the brook in the way: therefore shall he lift up the head.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). I will praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; with &#91;my&#93; whole heart, in the assembly of the upright, and &#91;in&#93; the congregation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> The works of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;are&#93; great, sought out of all them that have pleasure therein.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> His work &#91;is&#93; honourable and glorious: and his righteousness endures forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
