</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> The LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate) has chastened me sore: but he has not given me over unto death.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Open to me the gates of righteousness: I will go into them, &#91;and&#93; I will praise the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate):</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> This gate of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, into which the righteous shall enter.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> I will praise youi: for youi have heard me, and are become my salvation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> The stone &#91;which&#93; the builders refused is become the head &#91;stone&#93; of the corner.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> This is the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; doing; it &#91;is&#93; marvelous in our eyes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> This &#91;is&#93; the day &#91;which&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; has made; we will rejoice and be glad in it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Save now, I beseech (to call upon; appeal; beg) youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, I beseech (to call upon; appeal; beg) youi, send now prosperity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; he that cometh in the name of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: we have Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) you out of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, which has showed us light: bind the sacrifice with cords, &#91;even&#93; unto the horns of the altar.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> youi &#91;are&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and I will praise youi: &#91;youi are&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, I will exalt youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> O give thanks unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; for &#91;he is&#93; good: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> ALEPH. Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;are&#93; the undefiled in the way, who walk in the law of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;are&#93; they that keep his testimonies, &#91;and that&#93; seek him with the whole heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> They also do no iniquity: they walk in his ways.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> youi have commanded &#91;us&#93; to keep youri precepts diligently.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> O that my ways were directed to keep youri statutes!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Then shall I not be ashamed, when I have respect unto all youri commandments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> I will praise youi with uprightness of heart, when I shall have learned youri righteous judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> I will keep youri statutes: O forsake me not utterly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> BETH. Wherewithal shall a young man cleanse his way? by taking heed &#91;thereto&#93; according to youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> With my whole heart have I sought youi: O let me not wander from youri commandments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Your3s word have I hid in mine heart, that I might not sin against youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;are&#93; youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: teach me youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> With my lips have I declared all the judgments of youri mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> I have rejoiced in the way of youri testimonies, as &#91;much as&#93; in all riches.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> I will meditate in youri precepts, and have respect unto youri ways.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> I will delight myself in youri statutes: I will not forget youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> GIMEL. Deal bountifully with youri servant, &#91;that&#93; I may live, and keep youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> Open youi mine eyes, that I may behold wondrous things out of youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> I &#91;am&#93; a stranger in the earth: hide not youri commandments from me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> My soul breaks for the longing &#91;that it has&#93; unto youri judgments at all times.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> youi have rebuked the proud &#91;that are&#93; cursed, which do err from youri commandments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> Remove from me reproach (disgrace; shame) and contempt; for I have kept youri testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Princes also did sit &#91;and&#93; speak against me: &#91;but&#93; youri servant did meditate in youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> Your2s testimonies also &#91;are&#93; my delight &#91;and&#93; my counselors.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> DALETH. My soul cleaves unto the dust: quicken youi me according to youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> I have declared my ways, and youi heard me: teach me youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v27" data-v="27"> Make me to understand the way of youri precepts: so shall I talk of youri wondrous works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v28" data-v="28"> My soul melts for heaviness: strengthen youi me according unto youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v29" data-v="29"> Remove from me the way of lying: and grant me youri law graciously.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v30" data-v="30"> I have chosen the way of truth: youri judgments have I laid &#91;before me&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v31" data-v="31"> I have stuck unto youri testimonies: O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, put me not to shame.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v32" data-v="32"> I will run the way of youri commandments, when youi shall enlarge my heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v33" data-v="33"> HE. Teach me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, the way of youri statutes; and I shall keep it &#91;unto&#93; the end.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v34" data-v="34"> Give me understanding, and I shall keep youri law; yea, I shall observe it with &#91;my&#93; whole heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v35" data-v="35"> Make me to go in the path of youri commandments; for therein do I delight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v36" data-v="36"> Incline my heart unto youri testimonies, and not to covetousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v37" data-v="37"> Turn away mine eyes from beholding vanity; &#91;and&#93; quicken youi me in youri way.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v38" data-v="38"> Establish youri word unto youri servant, who &#91;is devoted&#93; to youri fear.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v39" data-v="39"> Turn away my reproach (disgrace; shame) which I fear: for youri judgments &#91;are&#93; good.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v40" data-v="40"> Behold, I have longed after youri precepts: quicken me in youri righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v41" data-v="41"> VAU. Let youri mercies come also unto me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, &#91;even&#93; youri salvation, according to youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v42" data-v="42"> So shall I have wherewith to answer him that reproaches me: for I trust in youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v43" data-v="43"> And take not the word of tutterly out of my mouth; for I have hoped in youri judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v44" data-v="44"> So shall I keep youri law continually forever and ever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v45" data-v="45"> And I will walk at liberty: for I seek youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v46" data-v="46"> I will speak of youri testimonies also before kings, and will not be ashamed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v47" data-v="47"> And I will delight myself in youri commandments, which I have loved.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v48" data-v="48"> My hands also will I lift up unto youri commandments, which I have loved; and I will meditate in youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v49" data-v="49"> ZAIN. Remember the word unto youri servant, upon which youi have caused me to hope.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v50" data-v="50"> This &#91;is&#93; my comfort in my affliction: for youri word has quickened me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v51" data-v="51"> The proud have had me greatly in ridicule (laughing at enemies’ threats): &#91;yet&#93; have I not declined from youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v52" data-v="52"> I remembered youri judgments of old, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and have comforted myself.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v53" data-v="53"> Horror has taken hold upon me because of the wicked that forsake youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v54" data-v="54"> Your2s statutes have been my songs in the house of my pilgrimage.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v55" data-v="55"> I have remembered youri name, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, in the night, and have kept you2ri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v56" data-v="56"> This I had, because I kept youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v57" data-v="57"> CHETH. &#91;youi are&#93; my portion, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: I have said that I would keep youri words.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v58" data-v="58"> I intreated youri favour with &#91;my&#93; whole heart: be merciful unto me according to youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v59" data-v="59"> I thought on my ways, and turned my feet unto youri testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v60" data-v="60"> I made haste, and delayed not to keep youri commandments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v61" data-v="61"> The bands of the wicked have robbed me: &#91;but&#93; I have not forgotten youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v62" data-v="62"> At midnight I will rise to give thanks unto youi because of youri righteous judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v63" data-v="63"> I &#91;am&#93; a companion of all &#91;them&#93; that fear youi, and of them that keep youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v64" data-v="64"> The earth, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, is full of youri mercy: teach me youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v65" data-v="65"> TETH. youi have dealt well with youri servant, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, according unto youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v66" data-v="66"> Teach me good judgment and knowledge: for I have believed youi commandments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v67" data-v="67"> Before I was afflicted I went astray: but now have I kept youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v68" data-v="68"> youi &#91;are&#93; good, and do good; teach me youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v69" data-v="69"> The proud have forged a lie against me: &#91;but&#93; I will keep youri precepts with &#91;my&#93; whole heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v70" data-v="70"> Their heart is as fat as grease; &#91;but&#93; I delight in youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v71" data-v="71"> &#91;It is&#93; good for me that I have been afflicted; that I might learn youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v72" data-v="72"> The law of youri mouth &#91;is&#93; better unto me than thousands of gold and silver.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v73" data-v="73"> JOD. Your2s hands have made me and fashioned me: give me understanding, that I may learn youri commandments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v74" data-v="74"> They that fear youi will be glad when they see me; because I have hoped in youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v75" data-v="75"> I know, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, that youri judgments &#91;are&#93; right, and &#91;that&#93; youri in faithfulness have afflicted me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v76" data-v="76"> Let, I pray youi, youri merciful kindness be for my comfort, according to youri word unto youri servant.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v77" data-v="77"> Let youri tender mercies come unto me, that I may live: for youri law &#91;is&#93; my delight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v78" data-v="78"> Let the proud be ashamed; for they dealt perversely with me without a cause: &#91;but&#93; I will meditate in youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v79" data-v="79"> Let those that fear youi turn unto me, and those that have known youri testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v80" data-v="80"> Let my heart be sound in youri statutes; that I be not ashamed.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v81" data-v="81"> CAPH. My soul faints for youri salvation: &#91;but&#93; I hope in youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v82" data-v="82"> Mine eyes fail for youri word, saying, When will youi comfort me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v83" data-v="83"> For I am become like a bottle in the smoke; &#91;yet&#93; do I not forget youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v84" data-v="84"> How many &#91;are&#93; the days of youri servant? when will youi execute judgment on them that persecute me?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v85" data-v="85"> The proud have digged pits for me, which &#91;are&#93; not after youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v86" data-v="86"> All youri commandments &#91;are&#93; faithful: they persecute me wrongfully; help youi me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v87" data-v="87"> They had almost consumed me upon earth; but I forsook not youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v88" data-v="88"> Quicken me after youri lovingkindness; so shall I keep the testimony of youri mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v89" data-v="89"> LAMED. Forever, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, youri word is settled in heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v90" data-v="90"> Your2s faithfulness &#91;is&#93; unto all generations: youi have established the earth, and it abides.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v91" data-v="91"> They continue this day according to youri ordinances: for all &#91;are&#93; youri servants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v92" data-v="92"> Unless youri law &#91;had been&#93; my delights, I should then have perished in mine affliction.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v93" data-v="93"> I will never forget yorui precepts: for with them youi have quickened me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v94" data-v="94"> I &#91;am&#93; yoursi, save me; for I have sought youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v95" data-v="95"> The wicked have waited for me to destroy me: &#91;but&#93; I will consider youri testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v96" data-v="96"> I have seen an end of all perfection: &#91;but&#93; youri commandment &#91;is&#93; exceeding broad.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v97" data-v="97"> MEM. O how love I youri law! it &#91;is&#93; my meditation all the day.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v98" data-v="98"> youi through youri commandments have made me wiser than mine enemies: for they &#91;are&#93; ever with me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v99" data-v="99"> I have more understanding than all my teachers: for youri testimonies &#91;are&#93; my meditation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v100" data-v="100"> I understand more than the ancients, because I keep youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v101" data-v="101"> I have refrained my feet from every evil way, that I might keep youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v102" data-v="102"> I have not departed from youri judgments: for youi have taught me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v103" data-v="103"> How sweet are youri words unto my taste! &#91;yea, sweeter&#93; than honey to my mouth!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v104" data-v="104"> Through youri precepts I get understanding: therefore I hate every false way.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v105" data-v="105"> NUN. Your2s word &#91;is&#93; a lamp unto my feet, and a light unto my path.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v106" data-v="106"> I have sworn, and I will perform &#91;it&#93;, that I will keep youri righteous judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v107" data-v="107"> I am afflicted very much: quicken me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, according unto youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v108" data-v="108"> Accept, I beseech (to call upon; appeal; beg) youi, the freewill offerings of my mouth, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and teach me youri judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v109" data-v="109"> My soul &#91;is&#93; continually in my hand: yet do I not forget youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v110" data-v="110"> The wicked have laid a snare for me: yet I erred not from youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v111" data-v="111"> Your2s testimonies have I taken as an heritage forever: for they &#91;are&#93; the rejoicing of my heart.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v112" data-v="112"> I have inclined mine heart to perform youri statutes always, &#91;even unto&#93; the end.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v113" data-v="113"> SAMECH. I hate &#91;vain&#93; thoughts: but youri law do I love.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v114" data-v="114"> youi &#91;are&#93; my hiding place and my shield: I hope in youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v115" data-v="115"> Depart from me, you2f evildoers: for I will keep the commandments of my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v116" data-v="116"> Uphold me according unto youri word, that I may live: and let me not be ashamed of my hope.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v117" data-v="117"> Hold youi me up, and I shall be safe: and I will have respect unto youri statutes continually.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v118" data-v="118"> youi have trampled down all them that err from youri statutes: for their deceit &#91;is&#93; falsehood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v119" data-v="119"> youi put away all the wicked of the earth &#91;like&#93; dross: therefore I love youri testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v120" data-v="120"> My flesh trembles for fear of youi; and I am afraid of youri judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v121" data-v="121"> AIN. I have done judgment and justice: leave me not to mine oppressors.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v122" data-v="122"> Be surety for youri servant for good: let not the proud oppress me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v123" data-v="123"> Mine eyes fail for youri salvation, and for the word of youri righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v124" data-v="124"> Deal with youri servant according unto youri mercy, and teach me youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v125" data-v="125"> I &#91;am&#93; youri servant; give me understanding, that I may know youri testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v126" data-v="126"> &#91;It is&#93; time for &#91;youi&#93;, LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, to work: &#91;for&#93; they have made void youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v127" data-v="127"> Therefore I love youri commandments above gold; yea, above fine gold.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v128" data-v="128"> Therefore I esteem all &#91;youi&#93; precepts &#91;concerning&#93; all &#91;things to be&#93; right; &#91;and&#93; I hate every false way.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v129" data-v="129"> PE. Your2s testimonies &#91;are&#93; wonderful: therefore does my soul keep them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v130" data-v="130"> The entrance of youri words gives light; it gives understanding unto the simple.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v131" data-v="131"> I opened my mouth, and panted: for I longed for youri commandments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v132" data-v="132"> Look youi upon me, and be merciful unto me, as youi used to do unto those that love youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v133" data-v="133"> Order my steps in youri word: and let not any iniquity have dominion over me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v134" data-v="134"> Deliver me from the oppression of man: so will I keep youi precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v135" data-v="135"> Make youri face to shine upon youri servant; and teach me youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v136" data-v="136"> Rivers of waters run down mine eyes, because they keep not youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v137" data-v="137"> TZADDI. Righteous &#91;are&#93; youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and upright &#91;are&#93; youri judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v138" data-v="138"> Your2s testimonies &#91;that&#93; youi have commanded &#91;are&#93; righteous and very faithful.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v139" data-v="139"> My zeal has consumed me, because mine enemies have forgotten youri words.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v140" data-v="140"> Your2s word &#91;is&#93; very pure: therefore youri servant loves it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v141" data-v="141"> I &#91;am&#93; small and despised: &#91;yet&#93; do not I forget youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v142" data-v="142"> Your2s righteousness &#91;is&#93; an everlasting righteousness, and youri law &#91;is&#93; the truth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v143" data-v="143"> Trouble and anguish have taken hold on me: &#91;yet&#93; youri commandments &#91;are&#93; my delights.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v144" data-v="144"> The righteousness of youri testimonies &#91;is&#93; everlasting: give me understanding, and I shall live.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v145" data-v="145"> KOPH. I cried with &#91;my&#93; whole heart; hear me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: I will keep youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v146" data-v="146"> I cried unto youi; save me, and I shall keep youri testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v147" data-v="147"> I prevented the dawning of the morning, and cried: I hoped in youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v148" data-v="148"> Mine eyes prevent (come before; go before; anticipate) the &#91;night&#93; watches, that I might meditate in youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v149" data-v="149"> Hear my voice according unto youri lovingkindness: O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, quicken me according to youri judgment.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v150" data-v="150"> They draw near that follow after mischief: they are far from youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v151" data-v="151"> youi &#91;are&#93; near, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and all youri commandments &#91;are&#93; truth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v152" data-v="152"> Concerning youri testimonies, I have known of old that youi have founded them forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v153" data-v="153"> RESH. Consider mine affliction, and deliver me: for I do not forget youri law.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v154" data-v="154"> Plead my cause, and deliver me: quicken me according to youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v155" data-v="155"> Salvation &#91;is&#93; far from the wicked: for they seek not youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v156" data-v="156"> Great &#91;are&#93; youri tender mercies, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: quicken me according to youri judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v157" data-v="157"> Many &#91;are&#93; my persecutors and mine enemies; &#91;yet&#93; do I not decline from youri testimonies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v158" data-v="158"> I beheld the transgressors, and was grieved; because they kept not youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v159" data-v="159"> Consider how I love youri precepts: quicken me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, according to youri lovingkindness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v160" data-v="160"> Your2s word &#91;is&#93; true &#91;from&#93; the beginning: and every one of youri righteous judgments &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v161" data-v="161"> SCHIN. Princes have persecuted me without a cause: but my heart stands in awe of youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v162" data-v="162"> I rejoice at youri word, as one that finds great spoil (plunder; prey; booty).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v163" data-v="163"> I hate and abhor lying: &#91;but&#93; youri law do I love.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v164" data-v="164"> Seven times a day do I praise youi because of youri righteous judgments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v165" data-v="165"> Great peace have they which love youri law: and nothing shall offend (make angry; make one stumble; violate) them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v166" data-v="166"> LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, I have hoped for youri salvation, and done youri commandments.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v167" data-v="167"> My soul has kept youri testimonies; and I love them exceedingly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v168" data-v="168"> I have kept youri precepts and youri testimonies: for all my ways &#91;are&#93; before youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v169" data-v="169"> TAU. Let my cry come near before youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: give me understanding according to youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v170" data-v="170"> Let my supplication come before youri: deliver me according to youri word.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v171" data-v="171"> My lips shall utter praise, when youi have taught me youri statutes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v172" data-v="172"> My tongue shall speak of youri word: for all youri commandments &#91;are&#93; righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v173" data-v="173"> Let youri hand help me; for I have chosen youri precepts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v174" data-v="174"> I have longed for youri salvation, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and youri law &#91;is&#93; my delight.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v175" data-v="175"> Let my soul live, and it shall praise youi; and let youri judgments help me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v176" data-v="176"> I have gone astray like a lost sheep; seek youri servant; for I do not forget youri commandments.<span className="desc">A Song of degrees.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> In my distress I cried unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and he heard me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Deliver my soul, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, from lying lips, &#91;and&#93; from a deceitful tongue.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> What shall be given unto youi? or what shall be done unto youi, youi false tongue?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Sharp arrows of the mighty, with coals of juniper.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Woe is me, that I sojourn in Meshekh &#91;Drawing Out&#93;, &#91;that&#93; I dwell in the tents of Keidar &#91;darkness&#93;!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> My soul has long dwelt with him that hates peace.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> I &#91;am for&#93; peace: but when I speak, they &#91;are&#93; for war.<span className="desc">A Song of degrees.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I will lift up mine eyes unto the hills, from whence cometh my help.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> My help &#91;cometh&#93; from the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, which made heaven and earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> He will not suffer youri foot to be moved: he that keeps youi will not slumber.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Behold, he that keeps Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; shall neither slumber nor sleep.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; youri keeper: the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; youi shade upon youri right hand.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> The sun shall not smite youi by day, nor the moon by night.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall preserve youi from all evil: he shall preserve youri soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall preserve youi going out and youri coming in from this time forth, and even forevermore.<span className="desc">A Song of degrees of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I was glad when they said unto me, Let us go into the house of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Our feet shall stand within youri gates, O Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93; is built as a city that is compact together:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> To where the tribes go up, the tribes of the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate), unto the testimony of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;, to give thanks unto the name of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> For there are set thrones of judgment, the thrones of the house of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Pray for the peace of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;: they shall prosper that love youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Peace be within youri walls, &#91;and&#93; prosperity within youri palaces.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> For my brethren and companions’ sakes, I will now say, Peace &#91;be&#93; within youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Because of the house of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; I will seek youri good.<span className="desc">A Song of degrees.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Unto youi lift I up mine eyes, O youi that dwell in the heavens.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Behold, as the eyes of servants &#91;look&#93; unto the hand of their masters, &#91;and&#93; as the eyes of a virgin unto the hand of her mistress; so our eyes &#91;wait&#93; upon the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, until that he have mercy upon us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Have mercy upon us, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, have mercy upon us: for we are exceedingly filled with contempt.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Our soul is exceedingly filled with the scorning of those that are at ease, &#91;and&#93; with the contempt of the proud.<span className="desc">A Song of degrees of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> If &#91;it had not been&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; who was on our side, now may Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; say;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> If &#91;it had not been&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; who was on our side, when men rose up against us:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Then they had swallow (to approve, commend, accept)ed us up quick, when their wrath was kindled against us:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Then the waters had overwhelmed us, the stream had gone over our soul:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Then the proud waters had gone over our soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, who has not given us &#91;as&#93; a prey to their teeth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Our soul is escaped as a bird out of the snare of the fowlers: the snare is broken, and we are escaped.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Our help &#91;is&#93; in the name of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, who made heaven and earth.<span className="desc">A Song of degrees.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> They that trust in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;shall be&#93; as mount Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;, &#91;which&#93; cannot be removed, &#91;but&#93; abides forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> As the mountains &#91;are&#93; round about Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;, so the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; round about his people from from this time forth (from now on) even forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> For the rod of the wicked shall not rest upon the Lot &#91;Covering&#93; of the righteous; lest the righteous put forth their hands unto iniquity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Do good, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, unto &#91;those that be&#93; good, and &#91;to them that are&#93; upright in their hearts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> As for such as turn aside unto their crooked ways, the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall lead them forth with the workers of iniquity: &#91;but&#93; peace &#91;shall be&#93; upon Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.<span className="desc">A Song of degrees.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> When the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; turned again the captivity of Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;, we were like them that dream.</span>

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Then was our mouth filled with laughter, and our tongue with singing: then said they among the heathen, The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; has done great things for them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; has done great things for us; &#91;whereof&#93; we are glad.</span>

<div className="bp">

</div>
<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">

<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> For the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will judge his people, and he will repent himself concerning his servants.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> The idols of the heathen &#91;are&#93; silver and gold, the work of men’s hands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> They have mouths, but they speak not; eyes have they, but they see not;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> They have ears, but they hear not; neither is there &#91;any&#93; breath in their mouths.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> They that make them are like unto them: &#91;so is&#93; every one that trusts in them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, O house of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;: bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, O house of Aharon &#91;Bright, Very High&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> Bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, O house of Levi &#91;Adhesion, Joiner&#93;: you2f that fear the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, bless the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) be the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; out of Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;, which dwells at Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;. Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
