<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> To him who alone does great wonders: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> To him that by wisdom made the heavens: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> To him that stretched out the earth above the waters: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> To him that made great lights: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> The sun to rule by day: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> The moon and stars to rule by night: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> To him that smote Mitzrayim &#91;Egypt - The Narrow Place&#93; in their firstborn: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> And brought out Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; from among them: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> With a strong hand, and with a stretched out arm: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> To him which divided the Red sea into parts: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> And made Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; to pass through the midst of it: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> But overthrew Par’oh &#91;Pharaoh - Great House&#93; and his host in the Red sea: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> To him which led his people through the wilderness: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> To him which smote great kings: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> And slew famous kings: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Sichon &#91;Warrior&#93; king of the Emori &#91;Highlanders&#93;: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> And ‘Og &#91;Long-necked&#93; the king of Bashan &#91;Fruitful&#93;: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> And gave their land for an heritage: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> &#91;Even&#93; an heritage unto Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; his servant: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Who remembered us in our low estate: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And has redeemed us from our enemies: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v25" data-v="25"> Who gives food to all flesh: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v26" data-v="26"> O give thanks unto the God-El Hashamayim &#91;The Powerful Living Word of Heaven&#93;: for his mercy &#91;endures&#93; forever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> By the rivers of Babylon, there we sat down, yea, we wept, when we remembered Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> We hanged our harps upon the willows in the midst thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> For there they that carried us away captive required of us a song; and they that wasted us &#91;required of us&#93; gladness, &#91;saying&#93;, Sing us &#91;one&#93; of the songs of Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> How shall we sing the LORD-Yehōvah’s &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; song in a strange land?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> If I forget youi, O Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;, let my right hand forget &#91;her cunning&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> If I do not remember youi, let my tongue cleave (split open; cut open; divide) to the roof of my mouth; if I prefer not Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93; above my chief joy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Remember, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, the children of Edom &#91;Red&#93; in the day of Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;; who said, Raze &#91;it&#93;, raze &#91;it, even&#93; to the foundation thereof.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> O daughter of Babylon, who are to be destroyed; happy &#91;shall he be&#93;, that rewards youi as youi have served us.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Happy &#91;shall he be&#93;, that takes and dashes youri little ones against the stones.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I will praise youi with my whole heart: before the gods will I sing praise unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> I will worship toward youri holy temple, and praise youi name for youri lovingkindness and for youri truth: for youi have magnified youri word above all youri name.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> In the day when I cried youi answered me, &#91;and&#93; strengthened me &#91;with&#93; strength in my soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> All the kings of the earth shall praise youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, when they hear the words of youri mouth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Yea, they shall sing in the ways of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: for great &#91;is&#93; the glory of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Though the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;be&#93; high, yet has he respect unto the lowly: but the proud he knows afar off.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Though I walk in the midst of trouble, youi will revive me: youi shall stretch forth youri hand against the wrath of mine enemies, and youri right hand shall save me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will perfect &#91;that which&#93; concerns me: youri mercy, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, &#91;endures&#93; forever: forsake not the works of youri own hands.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, youi have searched me, and known &#91;me&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> youi know my downsitting and mine uprising, youi understand my thought afar off.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> youi compass (go around, encircle; encompass) my path and my lying down, and are acquainted &#91;with&#93; all my ways.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> For &#91;there is&#93; not a word in my tongue, &#91;but&#93;, lo, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, youi know it altogether.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> youi have beset me behind and before, and laid youri hand upon me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> &#91;Such&#93; knowledge &#91;is&#93; too wonderful for me; it is high, I cannot &#91;attain&#93; unto it.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> To where shall I go from youri spirit? or to which shall I flee from youri presence?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> If I ascend up into heaven, youi &#91;are&#93; there: if I make my bed in hell, behold, youi &#91;are there&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> &#91;If&#93; I take the wings of the morning, &#91;and&#93; dwell in the uttermost parts of the sea;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Even there shall youri hand lead me, and youri right hand shall hold me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> If I say, Surely the darkness shall cover me; even the night shall be light about me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Yea, the darkness hides not from youi; but the night shines as the day: the darkness and the light &#91;are&#93; both alike &#91;to youi&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> For youi have possessed my reins (literally, kidneys; figuratively, seat of emotion): youi have covered me in my mother’s womb.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> I will praise youi; for I am fearfully &#91;and&#93; wonderfully made: marvelous &#91;are&#93; youri works; and &#91;that&#93; my soul knows right well.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> My substance was not hid from youi, when I was made in secret, &#91;and&#93; curiously wrought in the lowest parts of the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> Your2s eyes did see my substance, yet being unperfect; and in youri book all &#91;my members&#93; were written, &#91;which&#93; in continuance were fashioned, when &#91;as yet there was&#93; none of them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> How precious also are youri thoughts unto me, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;! how great is the sum of them!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> &#91;If&#93; I should count them, they are more in number than the sand: when I awake, I am still with youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> Surely youi will slay the wicked, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: depart from me therefore, you2f bloody men.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> For they speak against youi wickedly, &#91;and&#93; youri enemies take &#91;youri name&#93; in vain.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> Do not I hate them, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, that hate youi? and am not I grieved with those that rise up against youi?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v22" data-v="22"> I hate them with perfect hatred: I count them mine enemies.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v23" data-v="23"> Search me, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, and know my heart: try me, and know my thoughts:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v24" data-v="24"> And see if &#91;there be any&#93; wicked way in me, and lead me in the way everlasting.<span className="desc">To the Heavenly Worship Leader, A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Deliver me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, from the evil man: preserve me from the violent man;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Which imagine mischiefs in &#91;their&#93; heart; continually are they gathered together &#91;for&#93; war.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> They have sharpened their tongues like a serpent; adders’ poison &#91;is&#93; under their lips. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Keep me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, from the hands of the wicked; preserve me from the violent man; who have purposed to overthrow my goings.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> The proud have hid a snare for me, and cords; they have spread a net by the wayside; they have set gins for me. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> I said unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, youi &#91;are&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: hear the voice of my supplications, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> O GOD &#91;THE FATHER&#93; the Lord-Adonai &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;, the strength of my salvation, youi have covered my head in the day of battle.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Grant not, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, the desires of the wicked: further not his wicked device; &#91;lest&#93; they exalt themselves. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> &#91;As for&#93; the head of those that compass (go around, encircle; encompass) me about, let the mischief of their own lips cover them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Let burning coals fall upon them: let them be cast into the fire; into deep pits, that they rise not up again.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Let not an evil speaker be established in the earth: evil shall hunt the violent man to overthrow &#91;him&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> I know that the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; will maintain the cause of the afflicted, &#91;and&#93; the right of the poor.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Surely the righteous shall give thanks unto youri name: the upright shall dwell in youri presence.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, I cry unto youi: make haste unto me; give ear unto my voice, when I cry unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Let my prayer be set forth before youi &#91;as&#93; incense; &#91;and&#93; the lifting up of my hands &#91;as&#93; the evening sacrifice.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Set a watch, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, before my mouth; keep the door of my lips.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Incline not my heart to &#91;any&#93; evil thing, to practice wicked works with men that work iniquity: and let me not eat of their dainties.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Let the righteous smite me; &#91;it shall be&#93; a kindness: and let him reprove me; &#91;it shall be&#93; an excellent oil, &#91;which&#93; shall not break my head: for yet my prayer also &#91;shall be&#93; in their calamities.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> When their are overthrown in stony places, they shall hear my words; for they are sweet.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Our bones are scattered at the grave’s mouth, as when one cuts and cleaves &#91;wood&#93; upon the earth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> But mine eyes &#91;are&#93; unto youi, O GOD &#91;THE FATHER&#93; the Lord-Adonai (The Father) &#91;Greatest Above All Things&#93;: in youi is my trust; leave not my soul destitute.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Keep me from the snares &#91;which&#93; they have laid for me, and the gins of the workers of iniquity.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Let the wicked fall into their own nets, whilst that I withal escape.<span className="desc">Maskhil &#91;Contemplative poem&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;; A Prayer when he was in the cave.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I cried unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; with my voice; with my voice unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; did I make my supplication.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> I poured out my complaint before him; I showed before him my trouble.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> When my spirit was overwhelmed within me, then youi knew my path. In the way wherein I walked have they secretly laid a snare for me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> I looked on &#91;my&#93; right hand, and beheld, but &#91;there was&#93; no man that would know me: refuge failed me; no man cared for my soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> I cried unto youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: I said, youi &#91;are&#93; my refuge &#91;and&#93; my portion in the land of the living.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Attend unto my cry; for I am brought very low: deliver me from my persecutors; for they are stronger than I.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Bring my soul out of prison, that I may praise youri name: the righteous shall compass (go around, encircle; encompass) me about; for youi shall deal bountifully with me.<span className="desc">A Psalm (sacred song about or to God) of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Hear my prayer, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, give ear to my supplications: in youri faithfulness answer me, &#91;and&#93; in youri righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> And enter not into judgment with youri servant: for in youri sight shall no man living be justified.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> For the enemy has persecuted my soul; he has smitten my life down to the ground; he has made me to dwell in darkness, as those that have been long dead.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Therefore is my spirit overwhelmed within me; my heart within me is desolate.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> I remember the days of old; I meditate on all youri works; I muse on the work of youri hands.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> I stretch forth my hands unto youi: my soul &#91;thirsts&#93; after youi, as a thirsty land. Selah &#91;stop and diligently consider what was just said&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Hear me speedily, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: my spirit fails: hide not youri face from me, lest I be like unto them that go down into the pit.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Cause me to hear youri lovingkindness in the morning; for in youi do I trust: cause me to know the way wherein I should walk; for I lift up my soul unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Deliver me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, from mine enemies: I flee unto youi to hide me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Teach me to do youri will; for youi &#91;are&#93; my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: youri spirit &#91;is&#93; good; lead me into the land of uprightness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Quicken me, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, for youri name’s sake: for youri righteousness’ sake bring my soul out of trouble.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> And of youri mercy cut off mine enemies, and destroy all them that afflict my soul: for I &#91;am&#93; youri servant.<span className="desc">&#91;A Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of David &#91;Beloved&#93;.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) &#91;be&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; my strength, which teaches my hands to war, &#91;and&#93; my fingers to fight:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> My goodness, and my fortress; my high tower, and my deliverer; my shield, and &#91;he&#93; in whom I trust; who subdues my people under me.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, what &#91;is&#93; man, that youi take knowledge of him! &#91;or&#93; the son of man, that youi make account of him!</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Man is like to vanity: his days &#91;are&#93; as a shadow that passes away.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Bow youi heavens, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and come down: touch the mountains, and they shall smoke.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Cast forth lightning, and scatter them: shoot out youri arrows, and destroy them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Send youri hand from above; rid me, and deliver me out of great waters, from the hand of strange children;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Whose mouth speaks vanity, and their right hand &#91;is&#93; a right hand of falsehood.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> I will sing a new song unto youi, O God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;: upon a Psaltery (stringed instrument, like an harp) &#91;and&#93; an instrument of ten strings will I sing praises unto youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> &#91;It is he&#93; that gives salvation unto kings: who delivers David &#91;Beloved&#93; his servant from the hurtful sword.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Rid me, and deliver me from the hand of strange children, whose mouth speaks vanity, and their right hand &#91;is&#93; a right hand of falsehood:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> That our sons &#91;may be&#93; as plants grown up in their youth; &#91;that&#93; our daughters &#91;may be&#93; as corner stones, polished &#91;after&#93; the similitude of a palace:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> &#91;That&#93; our garners &#91;may be&#93; full, affording (providing, yealding (as of crops)) all manner of store: &#91;that&#93; our sheep may bring forth thousands and ten thousands in our streets:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> &#91;That&#93; our oxen &#91;may be&#93; strong to labour; &#91;that there be&#93; no breaking in, nor going out; that &#91;there be&#93; no complaining in our streets.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> Happy &#91;is that&#93; people, that is in such a case: &#91;yea&#93;, happy &#91;is that&#93; people, whose God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;.<span className="desc">David &#91;Beloved&#93;’s &#91;Psalm (sacred song about or to God)&#93; of praise.</span></span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> I will extol (to lift up; to praise; to esteem) youi, my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, O king; and I will bless youri name forever and ever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Every day will I bless youi; and I will praise youri name forever and ever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Great &#91;is&#93; the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, and greatly to be praised; and his greatness &#91;is&#93; unsearchable.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> One generation shall praise youi works to another, and shall declare youri mighty acts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> I will speak of the glorious honour of youri majesty, and of youri wondrous works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> And &#91;men&#93; shall speak of the might of youri terrible acts: and I will declare youri greatness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> They shall abundantly utter the memory of youri great goodness, and shall sing of youri righteousness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; gracious, and full of compassion; slow to anger, and of great mercy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; good to all: and his tender mercies &#91;are&#93; over all his works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> All youri works shall praise youi, O LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;; and youri saints shall bless youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> They shall speak of the glory of youri kingdom, and talk of youri power;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> To make known to the sons of men his mighty acts, and the glorious majesty of his kingdom.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Your2s kingdom &#91;is&#93; an everlasting kingdom, and youri dominion &#91;endures&#93; throughout all generations.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; uphold all that fall, and raises up all &#91;those that be&#93; bowed down.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> The eyes of all wait upon youi; and youi give them their meat in due season.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> youi open youri hand, and satisfy the desire of every living thing.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; righteous in all his ways, and holy in all his works.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; &#91;is&#93; near unto all them that call upon him, to all that call upon him in truth.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> He will fulfill the desire of them that fear him: he also will hear their cry, and will save them.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; preserves all them that love him: but all the wicked will he destroy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v21" data-v="21"> My mouth shall speak the praise of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: and let all flesh bless his holy name forever and ever.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). Praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, O my soul.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> While I live will I praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: I will sing praises unto my God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; while I have any being.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Put not your trust in princes, &#91;nor&#93; in the son of man, in whom &#91;there is&#93; no help.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> His breath goes forth, he returns to his earth; in that very day his thoughts perish.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Happy &#91;is he&#93; that &#91;has&#93; the God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; of Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93; for his help, whose hope &#91;is&#93; in the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; his God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Which made heaven, and earth, the sea, and all that therein &#91;is&#93;: which keeps tforever:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Which executes judgment for the oppressed: which gives food to the hungry. The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; looses the prisoners:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; opens &#91;the eyes of&#93; the blind: the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; raises them that are bowed down: the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; loves the righteous:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; preserves the strangers; he relieves the fatherless and widow: but the way of the wicked he turns upside down.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; shall reign forever, &#91;even&#93; youri God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, O Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;, unto all generations. Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate): for &#91;it is&#93; good to sing praises unto our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;; for &#91;it is&#93; pleasant; &#91;and&#93; praise is comely (attractive; proper (one’s own; peculiar; particular); becoming).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; does build up Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;: he gathers together the outcasts of Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> He heals the broken in heart, and binds up their wounds.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> He tells the number of the stars; he calls them all by &#91;their&#93; names.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Great &#91;is&#93; our Lord-Adon &#91;Lord of the Earth&#93;, and of great power: his understanding &#91;is&#93; infinite.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; lifts up the meek: he castes the wicked down to the ground.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Sing unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; with thanksgiving; sing praise upon the harp unto our God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Who covers the heaven with clouds, who prepares rain for the earth, who makes grass to grow upon the mountains.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> He gives to the beast his food, &#91;and&#93; to the young ravens which cry.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> He delights not in the strength of the horse: he takes not pleasure in the legs of a man.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> The LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; takes pleasure in them that fear him, in those that hope in his mercy.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;, O Yerushalayim &#91;Foundation Of Peace&#93;; praise youi God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93;, O Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> For he has strengthened the bars of youri gates; he has Blessed (Favored by God; happy; prosperous) youri children within youi.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v14" data-v="14"> He makes peace &#91;in&#93; youri borders, &#91;and&#93; fills youi with the finest of the wheat.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v15" data-v="15"> He send forth his commandment &#91;upon&#93; earth: his word runs very swiftly.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v16" data-v="16"> He gives snow like wool: he scatters the hoarfrost like ashes.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v17" data-v="17"> He castes forth his ice like morsels: who can stand before his cold?</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v18" data-v="18"> He send out his word, and melts them: he causes his wind to blow, &#91;and&#93; the waters flow.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v19" data-v="19"> He shows his word unto Ya’akov &#91;Heel of God&#93;, his statutes and his judgments unto Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93;.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v20" data-v="20"> He has not dealt so with any nation: and &#91;as for his&#93; judgments, they have not known them. Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). Praise you2f the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; from the heavens: praise him in the heights.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Praise you2f him, all his angels: praise you2f him, all his hosts.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Praise you2f him, sun and moon: praise him, all you2f stars of light.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Praise him, you2f heavens of heavens, and you2f waters that &#91;be&#93; above the heavens.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Let them praise the name of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: for he commanded, and they were created.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> He has also established them forever and ever: he has made a decree which shall not pass.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> Praise the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; from the earth, you2f dragons, and all deeps:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> Fire, and hail; snow, and vapour; stormy wind fulfilling his word:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> Mountains, and all hills; fruitful trees, and all cedars:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v10" data-v="10"> Beasts, and all cattle; creeping things, and flying fowl:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v11" data-v="11"> Kings of the earth, and all people; princes, and all of the earth:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v12" data-v="12"> Both young men, and maidens; old men, and children:</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v13" data-v="13"> Let them praise the name of the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93;: for his name alone is excellent; his glory &#91;is&#93; above the earth and heaven.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">

</div>

<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). Sing unto the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; a new song, &#91;and&#93; his praise in the congregation of saints.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Let Yisra’el &#91;He Holds Onto The Heel of God&#93; rejoice in him that made him: let the children of Tziyon &#91;Parched Place&#93; be joyful in their King.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Let them praise his name in the dance: let them sing praises unto him with the timbrel and harp.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> For the LORD-Yehōvah &#91;Messiah Pre-Incarnate&#93; takes pleasure in his people: he will beautify the meek with salvation.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Let the saints be joyful in glory: let them sing aloud upon their beds.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> &#91;Let&#93; the high &#91;praises&#93; of God-Elōhīm (The Living Word) &#91;The Many Powered&#93; &#91;be&#93; in their mouth, and a two-edged sword in their hand;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v7" data-v="7"> To execute vengeance upon the heathen, &#91;and&#93; punishments upon the people;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v8" data-v="8"> To bind their kings with chains, and their nobles with fetters of Yir’on &#91;fearing&#93;;</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v9" data-v="9"> To execute upon them the judgment written: this honour have all his saints. Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v1" data-v="1" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 26:4–5; Mishlei - Proverbs 4:14–15; Mishlei - Proverbs 13:20"> Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). Praise God-El &#91;The Creator&#93; in his sanctuary: praise him in the Firmament (expanse or vault over the earth) of his power.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v2" data-v="2" data-xref="Joshua 1:8; Tehillim - Psalms 119:92; Tehillim - Psalms 119:11"> Praise him for his mighty acts: praise him according to his excellent greatness.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v3" data-v="3" data-xref="Jeremiah 17:8; Ezekiel 47:12; Tehillim - Psalms 92:14"> Praise him with the sound of the trumpet: praise him with the Psaltery (stringed instrument, like an harp) and harp.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v4" data-v="4" data-xref="Job 21:18; Gosel 3:12; Tehillim - Psalms 35:5"> Praise him with the timbrel and dance: praise him with stringed instruments and organs.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v5" data-v="5" data-xref="Tehillim - Psalms 5:5; Gosel 25:46; Malachi 3:18"> Praise him upon the loud cymbals: praise him upon the high sounding cymbals.</span>
</div>
<div className="bp">
<span className="v" id="v6" data-v="6" data-xref="2 Timothy 2:19; John 10:14; Mishlei - Proverbs 15:9"> Let everything that has breath praise the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate). Praise you2f the LORD- Yah &#91;Behold The Hand&#93; (Messiah Pre-Incarnate).</span>
</div>
</div>

</div>
